> DEVELOPER

## 任务：按 pre-ui-service-design.md 实施 UI 前全部 service 侧代码改动

  ### 直接合同
  - `codestable/features/pre-ui-service-design.md` — 本轮唯一实施依据，4 个 wave，25+ 改动项
  - `codestable/quality/pre-ui-implementation-checklist.md` — 待办清单（Phase A-D + Post-UI）

  ### 边界护栏（启动前必读）
  - `CLAUDE.md` — 重写总原则、目录职责、Electron 边界、Selector 不可变
  - `codestable/quality/rewrite-frontend-conventions.md` — 前端规范（验证时不违反）
  - `codestable/quality/rewrite-quality-rules.md` — 代码质量规则

  ### Wave 结构

  **Wave 0: Task-real 类型重组（30 项，26 文件，5-8h）**
  - W0-1 类型系统（ScheduleDriver/FieldVariation/StepRepeat/重组 TaskDefinition 等 11 项）
  - W0-2 循环引擎统一（runTask/createDriver/createStopGuard/executeRepeatableSend）
  - W0-3 ConditionRegistry 清理（移除旧单条件接口）
  - W0-4 Step 执行器对齐（buildSendRequest 对齐 SendRequest）
  - W0-5 Service 层适配
  - W0-6 Task G1-G3 合并（updateTask/elapsedMs 派生/stopAll 返回值）
  - W0-7 Adapter + State + Selector 跟随（20 个文件）
  - W0-8 测试适配（70+ 现有 + 新增测试）
  - W0-9 代码精简（FrameReader 删除等）
  - 详细设计见 `codestable/features/rewrite-task/task-real-design.md` 全文

  **Wave 1: 代码修复（A1-A7）**
  - A1 receive expression integration pass（4 个测试失败）
  - A2 task skip-step 全局测试隔离
  - A3-A5 connection 清理（selectors 删除/mapBridgeEvent 去重/clone.ts 删除）
  - A6 command-ingress status-timer.ts 删除
  - A7 task selector readonly 强化

  **Wave 2: Command-Ingress Service Gaps（C1-C3）**
  - C1 命令执行日志（CommandLogRecorder）
  - C2 测试工具原始接收数据（testDataRecorder）
  - C3 sendTestData 方法

  **Wave 3: 代码清理（B1-B5）**
  - B1 frame dead surface、B2 connection dead surface + 类型重复、B3 HighlightRule 可序列化、B4 command-ingress dead surface、B5 send doc 修正

  **Wave 4: 基础设施（D1-D7）**
  - D1 UnoCSS spacing scale、D2-D3 checklist 回写、D4-D5 文档更新、D6 report 补测试、D7 receive-display bridge 类型对齐

  ### 执行规则
  1. Wave 0 和 Wave 1-4 可并行（不同 feature）。Wave 0 最大，优先启动。
  2. 一次最多 3 个子 agent 并行。
  3. 每个 wave 完成后跑 `pnpm -C rewrite vitest run` + `pnpm -C rewrite lint`。
  4. 全部完成后跑 `pnpm -C rewrite build`。目标 0 FAIL / 0 lint error / build 通过。
  5. 每完成一个 wave 立即 commit，不攒。
  6. 不做 UI、不改 runtime wiring、不建 TimerService platform 实现（只定义接口）。
  7. task-real 完成后 UI 可以直接在新类型体系上实施（ScheduleDriver/FieldVariation/StepRepeat 全部可用）。

  ### 已知风险
  - task-real 涉及 26 文件，是最大改动块。如果上下文不够，分两轮：W0-1~W0-4 第一轮（类型+引擎），W0-5~W0-9 第二轮（适配+测试）。
  - `buildSendRequest` 对齐后，command-ingress 的 task-builder 也需要跟随（`buildSendFrameTask`/`buildReadFileAndSendTask` 用了旧字段名）。
  - send 的 `SendRequest` 当前仍保留 `fieldValues` 兼容字段，task-real 改为 `userFieldValues` 后 send 侧需确认兼容。

176	## Wave 1: 代码修复（Phase A）
177	
178	### A1. Receive 表达式集成 pass
179	
180	**问题**: `processReceiveBatch` 未接受 `expressionCache` 参数，未对字段执行表达式后处理。4 个测试失败。
181	
182	**修改**:
183	
184	```
185	文件: receive/core/processor.ts（或 receive/services/ 中 processor 所在位置）
186	```
187	
188	1. `ProcessReceiveBatchOptions` 新增可选字段 `expressionCache?: ExpressionCache`
189	2. `processReceiveBatch` 内部，帧匹配成功后、返回 `ReceiveFieldResult` 前，新增 expression pass:
190	   - 遍历 `outcome.fields`
191	   - 如果 field 无 `expressionConfig`：标记 `expressionApplied: false`
192	   - 如果 field 有 `expressionConfig`：从 cache 取编译结果 → evaluate → 用计算值替换 raw value → 标记 `expressionApplied: true`
193	   - 如果 evaluate 失败：保留 raw value + 标记 `expressionApplied: true` + `expressionError: message` + 追加 issue `receive.expression.evalFailed`
194	3. 如果 `expressionCache` 中有 compile 失败的帧：匹配后 `kind: 'config-error'` + issue `receive.expression.compileFailed`
195	
196	**`ReceiveFieldResult` 扩展**:
197	```typescript
198	interface ReceiveFieldResult {
199	  // ...existing fields
200	  expressionApplied: boolean;
201	  expressionError?: string;
202	}
203	```
204	
205	**依赖**: 无。Expression engine 已验收（112 测试全绿），`compileFrameExpressions` 已存在于测试文件中，需提升到 receive core 层。
206	
207	**测试**: 现有 4 个 expression-pass.spec.ts 测试会通过。

306	
307	### C1. Command-Ingress — 命令执行日志（CI-G1，原 C4）
308	
309	**问题**: UI 命令执行日志 DataTable 无数据源。
310	
311	**修改**:
312	
313	```
314	新建: command-ingress/core/command-log.ts
315	修改: command-ingress/services/command-ingress-service.ts
316	修改: command-ingress/core/types.ts（如需）
317	```
318	
319	1. 新增类型:
320	```typescript
321	export interface CommandLogEntry {
322	  readonly id: string;           // nanoid 或自增
323	  readonly timestamp: string;   // ISO
324	  readonly commandCode: number;
325	  readonly result: 'success' | 'error' | 'pending';
326	  readonly durationMs?: number;
327	  readonly error?: string;
328	}
329	```
330	
331	2. 新增 recorder:
332	```typescript
333	export interface CommandLogRecorder {
334	  record(entry: Omit<CommandLogEntry, 'id'>): CommandLogEntry;
335	  complete(id: string, patch: Partial<Pick<CommandLogEntry, 'result' | 'durationMs' | 'error'>>): void;
336	  getAll(): readonly CommandLogEntry[];
337	  clear(): void;
338	}
339	
340	export function createCommandLogRecorder(maxEntries?: number): CommandLogRecorder;
341	```
342	
343	- `maxEntries` 默认 1000，超出丢弃最旧条目
344	- 内部维护 `CommandLogEntry[]`，`getAll()` 返回 readonly 快照
345	
346	3. Service 接口新增:
347	```typescript
348	getCommandLog(): readonly CommandLogEntry[];
349	clearCommandLog(): void;
350	```
351	
352	4. Service 内部在 `onCommand` 回调中集成:
353	```typescript
354	// onCommand 开始时
355	const logEntry = logRecorder.record({
356	  timestamp: now(),
357	  commandCode: parsed.commandCode,
358	  result: 'pending',
359	});
360	
361	// settlement 回调中
362	logRecorder.complete(logEntry.id, {
363	  result: inst.lifecycle === 'completed' ? 'success' : 'error',
364	  durationMs: Date.parse(inst.completedAt ?? inst.failedAt ?? '') - Date.parse(logEntry.timestamp),
365	  error: inst.error,
366	});
367	```
368	
369	**测试**: 新增 `command-log.spec.ts` 测试 recorder 的 record/complete/getAll/clear/maxEntries 行为。

385	```typescript
386	getTestDataRecorder(): DataRecorder;
387	```
388	
389	4. 实现简单:
390	```typescript
391	const testDataRecorder = createDataRecorder();
392	
393	// 在 adapter.consume 调用链中或 onCommand 入口
394	function recordTestData(rawBytes: Uint8Array): void {
395	  testDataRecorder.record({
396	    id: nanoid(),
397	    timestamp: now(),
398	    rawBytes,
399	    direction: 'inbound',
400	  });
401	}
402	```
403	
404	**验证**: 现有 114 测试不受影响。

406	### C3. Command-Ingress — sendTestData 方法（CI-G3，原 C6）
407	
408	**问题**: 测试工具发送区无法发送 HEX 数据。
409	
410	**修改**:
411	
412	```
413	文件: command-ingress/services/command-ingress-service.ts
414	```
415	
416	1. Service 接口新增:
417	```typescript
418	sendTestData(hex: string, targetId: string): Promise<void>;
419	```
420	
421	2. 实现:
422	```typescript
423	async sendTestData(hex: string, targetId: string): Promise<void> {
424	  const bytes = hexToBytes(hex);  // hex string → Uint8Array
425	  await connectionService.writeTo(targetId, bytes);
426	}
427	```
428	
429	3. `hexToBytes` 放在 `command-ingress/core/` 或 `shared/`（如果其他 feature 也需要）。
430	
431	4. 依赖: `connectionService.writeTo` 是否已暴露？需确认 ConnectionService 接口。如果未暴露，需要在 connection service 新增 `writeTo(targetId: string, data: Uint8Array): Promise<void>`。
432

1	# task-real design
2	
3	> 日期：2026-05-09
4	> 状态：draft
5	> Lane：Lane B（单 feature design）
6	> 直接合同：`codestable/features/rewrite-task/task-real-brainstorm.md`
7	> Checklist：`codestable/features/rewrite-task/rewrite-task-checklist.yaml`
8	> 边界护栏：CLAUDE.md、`codestable/architecture/rewrite-target-structure.md`、`codestable/quality/rewrite-quality-rules.md`
9	
10	## 设计摘要
11	
12	task-real 将现有三种独立调度循环（timed/trigger/sequence）统一为 `ScheduleDriver` discriminated union + `stopCondition` + `step.repeat` 三层控制。主要变更：类型重组、ConditionRegistry 升级为 AND/OR 组合匹配、send step 增加 repeat 能力、`buildSendRequest` 对齐实际 `SendRequest` API。
13	
14	---
15	
16	## 1. 类型系统
17	
18	### 1.1 新增类型
19	
20	#### ScheduleDriver
21	
22	替代 `TaskSchedulingMode` + `TaskDefinition` 上的平铺调度字段（`intervalMs`、`triggerCondition`、`delayBeforeStartMs`、`cooldownMs`）。
23	
24	```typescript
25	type ScheduleDriver =
26	  | { readonly kind: 'immediate' }
27	  | { readonly kind: 'timer'; readonly intervalMs: number }
28	  | { readonly kind: 'event'; readonly conditions: readonly ConditionTerm[]; readonly cooldownMs?: number };
29	```
30	
31	旧→新映射：
32	
33	| 旧模式 | 新表达 |
34	|--------|--------|
35	| `'sequence'` + 无额外字段 | `{ kind: 'immediate' }` |
36	| `'timed'` + `intervalMs` | `{ kind: 'timer', intervalMs }` |
37	| `'trigger'` + `triggerCondition` + `cooldownMs` | `{ kind: 'event', conditions: [...], cooldownMs }` |
38	
39	来源：brainstorm 决策 1
40	
41	#### ConditionTerm
42	
43	扩展 `WaitCondition`，新增 `logicOperator`。
44	
45	```typescript
46	interface ConditionTerm {
47	  readonly frameId: string;
48	  readonly fieldId: string;
49	  readonly operator: ComparisonOperator;
50	  readonly threshold: string | number;
51	  readonly sourceId?: string;
52	  readonly logicOperator?: 'and' | 'or';  // 新增，默认 'and'
53	}
54	```
55	
56	字段一一对应 `WaitCondition`（core/types.ts:26-32），新增 `logicOperator`。
57	
58	`logicOperator` 语义：`conditions[i].logicOperator` 决定当前条件如何与 `conditions[0..i-1]` 的累积结果组合。`conditions[0].logicOperator` 无意义（被忽略）。默认 `'and'`。
59	
60	来源：brainstorm 决策 3，旧系统 `TriggerCondition.logicOperator` 证据（`sendInstances.ts:123`、`useSendTaskTriggerListener.ts:115-155`）
61	
62	#### FieldVariation
63	
64	```typescript
65	interface FieldVariation {
66	  readonly fieldId: string;
67	  readonly values: readonly (string | number)[];
68	}
69	```
70	
71	按迭代轮次覆盖 `SendStepConfig.userFieldValues` 中对应字段。`stopCondition.maxIterations` 自动设为 `max(fieldVariations[].values.length)`。
72	
73	来源：brainstorm 决策 6，旧系统 `readFileAndSend.ts` FieldVariation 翻译
74	
75	#### StepRepeat
76	
77	```typescript
78	interface StepRepeat {
79	  readonly intervalMs: number;
80	  readonly until?: readonly ConditionTerm[];
81	  readonly maxCount?: number;
82	}
83	```
84	
85	只用于 send step。来源：brainstorm 决策 2。
86	
87	### 1.2 变更类型
88	
89	#### TaskDefinition — major
90	
91	```typescript
92	interface TaskDefinition {
93	  readonly id: string;
94	  readonly name: string;
95	  readonly steps: readonly TaskStepDefinition[];
96	  readonly schedule: ScheduleDriver;                          // 替代 schedulingMode + 平铺字段
97	  readonly stopCondition?: TaskStopCondition;
98	  readonly fieldVariations?: readonly FieldVariation[];       // 新增
99	  readonly errorPolicy: TaskErrorPolicy;
100	}
101	```
102	
103	移除字段（对比 core/types.ts:113-126）：
104	
105	| 移除字段 | 原类型/位置 | 替代 |
106	|----------|------------|------|
107	| `schedulingMode` | `TaskSchedulingMode` | `schedule: ScheduleDriver` |
108	| `triggerSource` | `TaskTriggerSource` | 移除（信息性标识） |
109	| `targetId?` | `string` | 移到 `SendStepConfig.targetId` |
110	| `intervalMs?` | `number` | `ScheduleDriver.timer.intervalMs` |
111	| `delayBeforeStartMs?` | `number` | 首个 delay step |
112	| `triggerCondition?` | `WaitCondition` | `ScheduleDriver.event.conditions` |
113	| `cooldownMs?` | `number` | `ScheduleDriver.event.cooldownMs` |
114	
115	新增字段：`schedule`、`fieldVariations`。
116	
117	#### SendStepConfig — major
118	
119	```typescript
120	interface SendStepConfig {
121	  readonly frameId: string;
122	  readonly targetId: string;                                                    // 必需（原可选 + fallback definition.targetId）
123	  readonly userFieldValues?: Readonly<Record<string, string | number | boolean>>;  // 重命名 fieldValues → userFieldValues
124	  readonly variables?: VariableMap;                                             // 新增
125	  readonly intervalAfterMs?: number;                                            // 新增
126	  readonly repeat?: StepRepeat;                                                 // 新增
127	}
128	```
129	
130	对齐 `SendRequest`（send/core/types.ts:SendRequest）：
131	
132	| SendStepConfig 字段 | SendRequest 字段 | 说明 |
133	|--------------------|-----------------|------|
134	| `frameId` | `frameId: string` | 直接映射 |
135	| `targetId` | `targetId: string` | 直接映射（现必需） |
136	| `userFieldValues` | `userFieldValues?: Readonly<Record<string, SendFieldValue>>` | 对齐命名，`SendFieldValue = string \| number \| boolean` |
137	| `variables` | `variables?: VariableMap` | 直接映射，`VariableMap = ReadonlyMap<string, VariableValue>`（shared/expression/types.ts） |
138	| （构造） | `context?: SendContext` | 由 `buildSendRequest` 构造，不暴露给配置 |
139	| 移除 `options` | — | `SendRequest` 无此字段 |
140	| `intervalAfterMs` | — | task 内部消费 |
141	| `repeat` | — | task 内部消费 |
142	
143	#### WaitConditionStepConfig → WaitConditionConfig — major
144	
145	```typescript
146	interface WaitConditionConfig {
147	  readonly conditions: readonly ConditionTerm[];   // 单条件 → 条件数组
148	  readonly timeoutMs?: number;                     // 可选（undefined = 无限等待直到信号中断）
149	  readonly onTimeout: 'continue' | 'skip' | 'fail';
150	}
151	```
152	
153	变更（对比 core/types.ts:43-47）：
154	- `condition: WaitCondition` → `conditions: readonly ConditionTerm[]`
155	- `timeoutMs: number` → `timeoutMs?: number`
156	
157	#### TaskStepDefinition — minor
158	
159	```typescript
160	type TaskStepDefinition =
161	  | { readonly kind: 'send';    readonly id: string; readonly name?: string; readonly config: SendStepConfig }
162	  | { readonly kind: 'wait-condition'; readonly id: string; readonly name?: string; readonly config: WaitConditionConfig }
163	  | { readonly kind: 'delay';   readonly id: string; readonly name?: string; readonly config: DelayStepConfig };
164	```
165	
166	变更（对比 core/types.ts:53-77）：
167	- `sendConfig` → `config`，`waitConfig` → `config`，`delayConfig` → `config`
168	- `name?` 保留（display concern）
169	
170	#### TaskStopCondition — minor
171	
172	```typescript
173	interface TaskStopCondition {
174	  readonly maxIterations?: number;
175	  readonly maxDurationMs?: number;
176	  readonly exitCondition?: readonly ConditionTerm[];  // 新增
177	}
178	```
179	
180	对比 core/types.ts:106-109：新增 `exitCondition`。
181	
182	#### ConditionMatchInput — breaking
183	
184	```typescript
185	interface ConditionMatchInput {
186	  readonly frameId: string;
187	  readonly sourceId?: string;
188	  readonly fieldValues: Readonly<Record<string, number | string | null>>;  // 替代 fieldId + value
189	}
190	```
191	
192	对比 core/types.ts:223-228：移除 `fieldId`、`value`，新增 `fieldValues`。
193	
194	理由：AND/OR 组合条件需要一次评估多个字段。单字段 input 不满足需求。与旧系统行为一致（旧系统在每帧事件中评估所有条件，`useSendTaskTriggerListener.ts:115-155`）。
195	
196	### 1.3 移除类型
197	
198	| 移除项 | 原位置（core/types.ts） | 替代 |
199	|--------|----------------------|------|
200	| `TaskSchedulingMode` | :91 | `ScheduleDriver` |
201	| `TASK_SCHEDULING_MODES` | :90 | 无需常量 |
202	| `TaskTriggerSource` | :88 | 无替代 |
203	| `TASK_TRIGGER_SOURCES` | :81-87 | 无需常量 |
204	| `WaitCondition` | :26-32 | `ConditionTerm` |
205	| `WaitConditionStepConfig` | :43-47 | `WaitConditionConfig` |
206	| `SendStepConfig.options` | :40 | 移除 |
207	
208	### 1.4 不变类型
209	
210	| 类型 | 说明 |
211	|------|------|
212	| `TaskStepKind` | `'send' \| 'wait-condition' \| 'delay'` |
213	| `ComparisonOperator` | 完全不变，与 shared/condition-operators/types.ts 一致 |
214	| `TaskErrorPolicy` / `TaskErrorAction` | 完全不变 |
215	| `TaskLifecycleStatus` / `LifecycleAction` | 完全不变 |
216	| `SendStepResult` | 不变 |
217	| `WaitConditionStepResult` | 不变 |
218	| `DelayStepResult` | 不变 |
219	| `DelayStepConfig` | 不变 |
220	| `TaskStepResult` | 不变 |
221	
222	### 1.5 辅助函数
223	
224	```typescript
225	type ResolvedStopCondition = TaskStopCondition;
226	
227	function resolveStopCondition(def: TaskDefinition): ResolvedStopCondition {
228	  if (def.fieldVariations && def.fieldVariations.length > 0) {
229	    const maxLen = Math.max(...def.fieldVariations.map(v => v.values.length));
230	    return { ...def.stopCondition, maxIterations: maxLen };
231	  }
232	  return def.stopCondition ?? {};
233	}
234	```
235	
236	`resolveStopCondition` 放在 `core/types.ts` 中（与 TaskDefinition/TaskStopCondition 同文件），通过 `core/index.ts` 导出。
237	
238	---
239	
240	## 2. 循环引擎
241	
242	### 2.1 统一 runTask
243	
244	替代 `runTimedLoop`、`runTriggerLoop`、`runSequenceLoop`（task-iteration-loops.ts:106-233）。
245	
246	```typescript
247	interface TaskExecutionContext {
248	  readonly definition: TaskDefinition;
249	  readonly resolvedStop: ResolvedStopCondition;
250	  state: TaskStateContainer;
251	  stepExecutors: StepExecutors;
252	  conditionRegistry: ConditionRegistry;
253	  updateLifecycle: UpdateLifecycleFn;
254	  errorPolicy: ErrorPolicyHandler;
255	  fieldValueProvider?: () => Readonly<Record<string, number | string | null>>;
256	  timerService?: TimerService;
257	  now: () => string;
258	}
259	
260	async function runTask(
261	  instanceId: string,
262	  ctx: TaskExecutionContext,
263	  signal: Promise<void>,
264	): Promise<void> {
265	  const driver = createDriver(ctx.definition.schedule, ctx, instanceId, signal);
266	  const stopGuard = createStopGuard(ctx.resolvedStop, ctx);
267	
268	  let iteration = ctx.state.getInstance(instanceId)?.currentIteration ?? 0;
269	
270	  while (!stopGuard.shouldStop(instanceId, iteration)) {
271	    const triggered = await driver.waitForNext(instanceId, iteration, signal);
272	    if (!triggered) break;
273	
274	    const completed = await executeSteps(instanceId, iteration, ctx, signal);
275	    if (!completed) break;
276	
277	    if (stopGuard.checkExitCondition()) break;
278	
279	    iteration++;
280	    ctx.state.updateInstance(instanceId, { currentIteration: iteration });
281	  }
282	
283	  driver.dispose();
284	}
285	```
286	
287	### 2.2 ScheduleDriver 实现
288	
289	```typescript
290	interface ScheduleDriverAdapter {
291	  waitForNext(instanceId: string, iteration: number, signal: Promise<void>): Promise<boolean>;
292	  dispose(): void;
293	}
294	```
295	
296	#### immediate
297	
298	```typescript
299	{ waitForNext: async () => true, dispose: () => {} }
300	```
301	
302	#### timer
303	
304	```typescript
305	function createTimerDriver(intervalMs: number): ScheduleDriverAdapter {
306	  let firstCall = true;
307	
308	  return {
309	    async waitForNext(_id, _iter, signal) {
310	      if (firstCall) { firstCall = false; return true; }
311	
312	      const delay = sleep(intervalMs);
313	      const race = await Promise.race([
314	        delay.promise.then(() => true),
315	        signal.then(() => false),
316	      ]);
317	      if (!race) delay.cancel();
318	      return race;
319	    },
320	    dispose() {},
321	  };
322	}
323	```
324	
325	对比现有 `runTimedLoop`（task-iteration-loops.ts:106-158）：
326	- 首次调用无延迟（替代原 `delayBeforeStartMs`，现由 delay step 表达）
327	- 后续调用等待 intervalMs
328	- 通过 signal 支持中断
329	
330	#### event
331	
332	```typescript
333	function createEventDriver(
334	  schedule: Extract<ScheduleDriver, { kind: 'event' }>,
335	  ctx: TaskExecutionContext,
336	  instanceId: string,
337	  signal: Promise<void>,
338	): ScheduleDriverAdapter {
339	  const { conditions, cooldownMs = 0 } = schedule;
340	  let lastTriggerTime = 0;
341	  let notifyResolve: ((value: boolean) => void) | null = null;
342	  let disposed = false;
343	
344	  const group = ctx.conditionRegistry.registerGroup(conditions, () => {
345	    if (disposed) return;
346	    const now = Date.now();
347	    if (cooldownMs > 0 && lastTriggerTime > 0 && now - lastTriggerTime < cooldownMs) return;
348	    lastTriggerTime = now;
349	    if (notifyResolve) { notifyResolve(true); notifyResolve = null; }
350	  });
351	
352	  return {
353	    async waitForNext() {
354	      if (disposed) return false;
355	      return Promise.race([
356	        new Promise<boolean>((resolve) => { notifyResolve = resolve; }),
357	        signal.then(() => false),
358	      ]);
359	    },
360	    dispose() {
361	      disposed = true;
362	      ctx.conditionRegistry.unregisterGroup(group);
363	      if (notifyResolve) { notifyResolve(false); notifyResolve = null; }
364	    },
365	  };
366	}
367	```
368	
369	对比现有 `runTriggerLoop`（task-iteration-loops.ts:160-223）：
370	- 从 `register(condition, handler)` → `registerGroup(conditions, handler)`
371	- cooldownMs 逻辑从循环体内移到 driver 内部
372	- triggerQueue 机制简化为 Promise resolve
373	
374	### 2.3 StopGuard
375	
376	```typescript
377	interface StopGuard {
378	  shouldStop(instanceId: string, iteration: number): boolean;
379	  checkExitCondition(): boolean;
380	}
381	
382	function createStopGuard(stop: ResolvedStopCondition, ctx: TaskExecutionContext): StopGuard {
383	  const startTime = Date.now();
384	
385	  return {
386	    shouldStop(instanceId, iteration) {
387	      const inst = ctx.state.getInstance(instanceId);
388	      if (!inst || inst.lifecycle !== 'running') return true;
389	      if (stop.maxIterations !== undefined && iteration >= stop.maxIterations) return true;
390	      if (stop.maxDurationMs !== undefined && Date.now() - startTime >= stop.maxDurationMs) return true;
391	      return false;
392	    },
393	
394	    checkExitCondition() {
395	      if (!stop.exitCondition) return false;
396	      if (!ctx.fieldValueProvider) return false;
397	      return evaluateConditionGroup(stop.exitCondition, ctx.fieldValueProvider());
398	    },
399	  };
400	}
401	```
402	
403	`checkExitCondition` 需要 `fieldValueProvider`（由 runtime 层从 `ReceiveReader.listFieldValues()` 提供，测试用 fake）。
404	
405	### 2.4 step repeat
406	
407	```typescript
408	async function executeRepeatableSend(
409	  ctx: TaskExecutionContext,
410	  instanceId: string,
411	  step: SendStepDefinition,
412	  iteration: number,
413	  stepIndex: number,
414	  signal: Promise<void>,
415	): Promise<boolean> {
416	  const { repeat } = step.config;
417	  if (!repeat) {
418	    const result = await ctx.stepExecutors.executeSendStep(instanceId, step, iteration, stepIndex);
419	    ctx.state.addStepResult(instanceId, result);
420	    return result.sendResult.kind === 'sent';
421	  }
422	
423	  let count = 0;
424	  const maxCount = repeat.maxCount ?? Infinity;
425	
426	  while (count < maxCount) {
427	    const result = await ctx.stepExecutors.executeSendStep(instanceId, step, iteration, stepIndex);
428	    ctx.state.addStepResult(instanceId, result);
429	
430	    if (result.sendResult.kind !== 'sent') return false;
431	
432	    count++;
433	
434	    if (repeat.until && ctx.fieldValueProvider) {
435	      if (evaluateConditionGroup(repeat.until, ctx.fieldValueProvider())) break;
436	    }
437	
438	    if (count < maxCount) {
439	      const delay = sleep(repeat.intervalMs);
440	      const race = await Promise.race([delay.promise.then(() => true), signal.then(() => false)]);
441	      if (!race) { delay.cancel(); return false; }
442	    }
443	  }
444	
445	  return true;
446	}
447	```
448	
449	### 2.5 fieldVariations 注入
450	
451	在 `buildSendRequest` 时合并，不是单独的注入步骤。
452	
453	```typescript
454	function resolveFieldValues(
455	  baseValues: Readonly<Record<string, string | number | boolean>> | undefined,
456	  fieldVariations: readonly FieldVariation[] | undefined,
457	  iteration: number,
458	): Readonly<Record<string, string | number | boolean>> {
459	  if (!fieldVariations || fieldVariations.length === 0) return baseValues ?? {};
460	
461	  const result: Record<string, string | number | boolean> = { ...(baseValues ?? {}) };
462	  for (const variation of fieldVariations) {
463	    if (iteration < variation.values.length) {
464	      result[variation.fieldId] = variation.values[iteration];
465	    }
466	  }
467	  return result;
468	}
469	```
470	
471	---
472	
473	## 3. ConditionRegistry 升级
474	
475	### 3.1 evaluateConditionGroup
476	
477	替代现有 `evaluateCondition`（core/condition-matcher.ts），支持 AND/OR 组合。
478	
479	```typescript
480	function evaluateConditionGroup(
481	  conditions: readonly ConditionTerm[],
482	  fieldValues: Readonly<Record<string, number | string | null>>,
483	): boolean {
484	  if (conditions.length === 0) return true;
485	
486	  let result = evaluateSingleCondition(conditions[0], fieldValues);
487	
488	  for (let i = 1; i < conditions.length; i++) {
489	    const op = conditions[i].logicOperator ?? 'and';
490	    const current = evaluateSingleCondition(conditions[i], fieldValues);
491	
492	    if (op === 'or') {
493	      result = result || current;
494	      if (result) break;   // OR 短路
495	    } else {
496	      result = result && current;
497	      if (!result) break;  // AND 短路
498	    }
499	  }
500	
501	  return result;
502	}
503	
504	function evaluateSingleCondition(
505	  condition: ConditionTerm,
506	  fieldValues: Readonly<Record<string, number | string | null>>,
507	): boolean {
508	  const value = fieldValues[condition.fieldId];
509	  if (value === undefined || value === null) return false;
510	  return compareValues(value, condition.threshold, condition.operator);
511	}
512	```
513	
514	来源：旧系统 `useSendTaskTriggerListener.ts:115-154` AND/OR 短路逻辑
515	依赖：`compareValues`（shared/condition-operators/index.ts:3）
516	
517	### 3.2 ConditionRegistry 改造
518	
519	从单条件注册（condition-registry.ts:11-56）改为条件组注册。
520	
521	```typescript
522	interface ConditionGroup {
523	  readonly id: string;
524	  readonly conditions: readonly ConditionTerm[];
525	  readonly onSatisfied: () => void;
526	}
527	
528	class ConditionRegistry {
529	  private groups = new Map<string, ConditionGroup>();
530	  private frameIndex = new Map<string, Set<string>>();
531	  private nextGroupId = 1;
532	
533	  registerGroup(conditions: readonly ConditionTerm[], onSatisfied: () => void): ConditionGroup {
534	    const id = `cg-${this.nextGroupId++}`;
535	    const group: ConditionGroup = { id, conditions, onSatisfied };
536	    this.groups.set(id, group);
537	
538	    const frameIds = new Set(conditions.map(c => c.frameId));
539	    for (const frameId of frameIds) {
540	      if (!this.frameIndex.has(frameId)) this.frameIndex.set(frameId, new Set());
541	      this.frameIndex.get(frameId)!.add(id);
542	    }
543	
544	    return group;
545	  }
546	
547	  unregisterGroup(group: ConditionGroup): void {
548	    this.groups.delete(group.id);
549	    for (const set of this.frameIndex.values()) set.delete(group.id);
550	  }
551	
552	  processInput(input: ConditionMatchInput): void {
553	    const groupIds = this.frameIndex.get(input.frameId);
554	    if (!groupIds) return;
555	
556	    for (const groupId of groupIds) {
557	      const group = this.groups.get(groupId);
558	      if (!group) continue;
559	
560	      // sourceId 过滤
561	      if (input.sourceId !== undefined) {
562	        const hasSourceMatch = group.conditions.some(
563	          c => c.sourceId === undefined || c.sourceId === input.sourceId
564	        );
565	        if (!hasSourceMatch) continue;
566	      }
567	
568	      if (evaluateConditionGroup(group.conditions, input.fieldValues)) {
569	        group.onSatisfied();
570	      }
571	    }
572	  }
573	
574	  clear(): void {
575	    this.groups.clear();
576	    this.frameIndex.clear();
577	  }
578	
579	  get size(): number {
580	    return this.groups.size;
581	  }
582	}
583	```
584	
585	### 3.3 ConditionMatchInput 变更影响
586	
587	| 消费方 | 文件 | 影响 |
588	|--------|------|------|
589	| `ConditionRegistry.processInput` | services/condition-registry.ts | 签名不变，内部改为 group 评估 |
590	| `evaluateCondition` | core/condition-matcher.ts | 签名变更 |
591	| `fake-receive-event-source.ts` | adapters/ | 适配新 `ConditionMatchInput` |
592	| 测试 fixtures | fixtures/ | 适配新结构 |
593	
594	### 3.4 跨 feature 协调说明
595	
596	`ConditionMatchInput` 变更是 **task 内部变更**，不影响其他 feature 的公开 API。
597	
598	**数据流**：
599	```
600	receive-real (公开 API 不变)
601	  → runtime 层 ReceiveEventSource 适配器 (需更新)
602	    → task ConditionRegistry.processInput (内部变更)
603	```
604	
605	- `ConditionMatchInput` 定义在 `task/core/types.ts`，是 task feature 的内部类型
606	- `ReceiveEventSource` 接口定义在 `task/adapters/ports.ts`，也是 task 内部
607	- **receive-real 不需要任何变更**。receive 的 `ReceiveReader.listFieldValues()` / `ReceiveService.listEvents()` 公开 API 完全不变
608	- 需要变更的是 **runtime 层**的 `ReceiveEventSource` 适配器实现：从逐字段 emit 改为逐帧 emit（将 `ReceiveFieldValueSnapshot[]` 按 frameId 聚合为 `ConditionMatchInput.fieldValues`）
609	- **send-real 不需要任何变更**。`SendRequest` / `SendService.execute()` 公开 API 不变，是 task 的 `buildSendRequest` 对齐到已有的 `SendRequest` 签名
610	
611	**实施节奏**：task-real 可以独立完成所有变更（类型、引擎、测试），runtime 适配器在 runtime 装配阶段更新。不阻塞 task-real 实施。
612	
613	---
614	
615	## 4. Step 执行器
616	
617	### 4.1 buildSendRequest 对齐
618	
619	当前代码（task-step-executors.ts:17-29）与 `SendRequest`（send/core/types.ts）不对齐：
620	
621	| 当前代码 | SendRequest 实际字段 | 修正 |
622	|----------|---------------------|------|
623	| `stepConfig.sendConfig.fieldValues` | `userFieldValues?: Readonly<Record<string, SendFieldValue>>` | 重命名 |
624	| `stepConfig.sendConfig.targetId ?? definition.targetId ?? ''` | `targetId: string`（必需） | 移除 fallback |
625	| `stepConfig.sendConfig.options ?? {}` | 不存在 | 移除 |
626	| 缺失 | `variables?: VariableMap` | 新增 |
627	
628	修正后：
629	
630	```typescript
631	function buildSendRequest(
632	  stepConfig: SendStepConfig,
633	  definition: TaskDefinition,
634	  stepIndex: number,
635	  iteration: number,
636	): SendRequest {
637	  return {
638	    frameId: stepConfig.frameId,
639	    targetId: stepConfig.targetId,
640	    userFieldValues: resolveFieldValues(
641	      stepConfig.userFieldValues,
642	      definition.fieldVariations,
643	      iteration,
644	    ),
645	    variables: stepConfig.variables,
646	    context: { source: 'task', taskId: definition.id, stepIndex },
647	  };
648	}
649	```
650	
651	### 4.2 wait-condition step 执行器
652	
653	从注册单个 `WaitCondition` 改为注册条件组。
654	
655	```typescript
656	async function executeWaitConditionStep(
657	  instanceId: string,
658	  step: WaitConditionStepDefinition,
659	  iteration: number,
660	  stepIndex: number,
661	  signal: Promise<void>,
662	): Promise<{ result: TaskStepResult; interrupted: boolean }> {
663	  const { conditions, timeoutMs, onTimeout } = step.config;
664	
665	  return new Promise((resolve) => {
666	    let settled = false;
667	
668	    const group = ctx.conditionRegistry.registerGroup(conditions, () => {
669	      if (settled) return;
670	      settled = true;
671	      ctx.conditionRegistry.unregisterGroup(group);
672	      if (timer !== undefined) clearTimeout(timer);
673	      resolve({
674	        result: { kind: 'wait-condition', stepIndex, iteration, matched: true, timedOut: false },
675	        interrupted: false,
676	      });
677	    });
678	
679	    const timer = timeoutMs !== undefined
680	      ? setTimeout(() => {
681	          if (settled) return;
682	          settled = true;
683	          ctx.conditionRegistry.unregisterGroup(group);
684	          resolve({
685	            result: { kind: 'wait-condition', stepIndex, iteration, matched: false, timedOut: true },
686	            interrupted: false,
687	          });
688	        }, timeoutMs)
689	      : undefined;
690	
691	    signal.then(() => {
692	      if (settled) return;
693	      settled = true;
694	      ctx.conditionRegistry.unregisterGroup(group);
695	      if (timer !== undefined) clearTimeout(timer);
696	      resolve({
697	        result: { kind: 'wait-condition', stepIndex, iteration, matched: false, timedOut: false },
698	        interrupted: true,
699	      });
700	    });
701	  });
702	}
703	```
704	
705	### 4.3 delay step 执行器
706	
707	无变更。现有 `executeDelayStep`（task-step-executors.ts:117-135）已满足需求。
708	
709	### 4.4 intervalAfterMs 处理
710	
711	在 `executeSteps` 循环中，send step 执行后检查 `intervalAfterMs`：
712	
713	```typescript
714	// 在 executeSteps 的 step 循环内：
715	if (step.kind === 'send' && step.config.intervalAfterMs) {
716	  const delay = sleep(step.config.intervalAfterMs);
717	  const race = await Promise.race([delay.promise.then(() => 'done'), signal.then(() => 'intr')]);
718	  if (race === 'intr') { delay.cancel(); return false; }
719	}
720	```
721

724	## 5. TimerService adapter
725	
726	### 5.1 接口定义
727	
728	在 `adapters/ports.ts` 中新增：
729	
730	```typescript
731	export interface TimerService {
732	  now(): number;
733	  setTimeout(callback: () => void, delayMs: number): number;
734	  clearTimeout(id: number): void;
735	  setInterval(callback: () => void, intervalMs: number): number;
736	  clearInterval(id: number): void;
737	}
738	```
739	
740	### 5.2 注入方式
741	
742	```typescript
743	export interface CreateTaskServiceOptions {
744	  readonly sendService: SendServiceProvider;
745	  readonly receiveEventSource: ReceiveEventSource;
746	  readonly timerService?: TimerService;       // 可选
747	  readonly fieldValueProvider?: () => Readonly<Record<string, number | string | null>>;  // 新增
748	  readonly state?: TaskStateContainer;
749	  readonly now?: () => string;
750	}
751	```
752	
753	### 5.3 实现位置
754	
755	| 组件 | 位置 |
756	|------|------|
757	| 接口定义 | `task/adapters/ports.ts` |
758	| platform 实现 | `rewrite/src/platform/` 下（Web Worker，不阻塞 task-real） |
759	| fake 实现 | `task/adapters/fake-timer-service.ts` |
760	
761	### 5.4 当前策略
762	
763	`sleep()` 继续使用标准 `setTimeout`。TimerService 作为可选注入，后续替换。不阻塞 task-real 实施。
764	
765	---
766	
767	## 6. 状态管理
768	
769	### 6.1 TaskInstanceState
770	
771	无结构性变更。`definitionRef` 类型从旧 `TaskDefinition` 更新为新 `TaskDefinition`。
772	
773	### 6.2 stepResults 上限
774	
775	长期运行任务可能产生大量 stepResults。
776	
777	策略：保留最近 N 条（默认 100，可通过 `CreateTaskServiceOptions` 配置）。超出时丢弃最旧的结果。不影响进度计算（进度基于计数器而非 stepResults 数组长度）。
778	
779	### 6.3 进度计算
780	
781	`calculateProgress`（core/progress.ts）逻辑不变，适配新类型即可。`iterationsTotal` 从 `resolvedStop.maxIterations` 计算。
782

903	
904	## 8. 翻译约定
905	
906	### 8.1 startDelay → delay step
907	
908	旧 `delayBeforeStartMs` → steps 首位插入 delay step。由 UI 或 SCOE adapter 在构造 TaskDefinition 时处理。
909	
910	### 8.2 responseDelay → delay step
911	
912	旧触发后 `responseDelay` → event schedule 的 steps 首位插入 delay step。
913	
914	### 8.3 SCOE 参数 resolve
915	
916	SCOE adapter 翻译时将参数值 resolve 为 literal：
917	- 参数值 → `SendStepConfig.userFieldValues`
918	- 完成条件阈值 → `ConditionTerm.threshold`
919	- task 引擎不理解参数关联
920	
921	### 8.4 文件发送
922	
923	SCOE adapter 读取文件 → `FieldVariation[]`：
924	- `.txt`：按行分割，过滤空行
925	- 非 `.txt`：逗号分隔
926	- `maxLength = max(values.length)` → 自动 `stopCondition.maxIterations`
927	
928	### 8.5 单帧发送
929	
930	```typescript
931	{
932	  id: generateId(),
933	  name: 'single-send',
934	  steps: [{ kind: 'send', id: 'step-0', config: { frameId, targetId, userFieldValues } }],
935	  schedule: { kind: 'immediate' },
936	  errorPolicy: { onFailure: 'stop' },
937	}
938	```
939

1007	## 10. 不确定事项
1008	
1009	| # | 事项 | 影响 | 建议 |
1010	|---|------|------|------|
1011	| 1 | `cooldownMs` 是否加入 `ScheduleDriver.event` | event driver 触发频率控制 | 已加入为可选字段，旧系统有完整证据 |
1012	| 2 | `exitCondition` / `repeat.until` 的同步评估需要当前 receive field values | StopGuard 和 repeat.until 需要 | 通过 `fieldValueProvider` 注入，runtime 连接 ReceiveReader |
1013	| 3 | TimerService platform 实现位置 | 不阻塞设计 | 只定义接口，实现由 platform 承接 |
1014	| 4 | 不同 frameId 的条件在同组中如何处理 | 边界情况 | 当前实际场景中同组条件引用同一 frameId；跨 frame 场景留后续处理 |
1015	| 5 | `fieldVariations.fieldId` 是否需要 stepId 或 frameId 区分同字段不同 step | 可能歧义 | 当前 fieldVariations 按 fieldId 覆盖所有 send step 的 userFieldValues；per-step 粒度后续可扩展 |

1	import {
2	  bytesToReadableHex,
3	  normalizeByteArray,
4	  receiveIssue,
5	} from './bytes';
6	import {
7	  cloneFrameReference,
8	  cloneInputBatch,
9	  cloneInputError,
10	  cloneIssue,
11	  cloneParsedField,
12	} from './clone';
13	import { parseReceiveFrameFields } from './field-parser';
14	import { matchReceiveFrame } from './frame-matcher';
15	import { evaluateFrameExpressions } from './expression-pass';
16	import type {
17	  ReceiveBatchOutcome,
18	  ReceiveFrameStatisticsSnapshot,
19	  ReceiveInputBatch,
20	  ReceiveInputError,
21	  ReceiveIssue,
22	  ReceiveMatchedFrameSummary,
23	  ReceiveOutcomeKind,
24	  ReceiveParsedFieldValue,
25	  ReceiveProcessInput,
26	  ReceiveSourceSnapshot,
27	  ReceiveSourceStatisticsSnapshot,
28	  ReceiveStatsDelta,
29	} from './types';
30	
31	function matchedFrameSummary(
32	  frameId: string,
33	  frameName: string,
34	  byteLength: number,
35	  fieldCount: number,
36	): ReceiveMatchedFrameSummary {
37	  return {
38	    frameId,
39	    frameName,
40	    byteLength,
41	    fieldCount,
42	  };
43	}
44	
45	function sourceHit(
46	  source: ReceiveSourceSnapshot,
47	  kind: ReceiveOutcomeKind,
48	  byteLength: number,
49	  occurredAt: string,
50	): ReceiveSourceStatisticsSnapshot {
51	  return {
52	    sourceId: source.sourceId,
53	    connectionId: source.connectionId,
54	    kind: source.kind,
55	    label: source.label,
56	    batchCount: kind === 'input-error' ? 0 : 1,
57	    byteCount: kind === 'input-error' || kind === 'stale-input' ? 0 : byteLength,
58	    matchedCount: kind === 'matched' ? 1 : 0,
59	    unmatchedCount: kind === 'unmatched' ? 1 : 0,
60	    configErrorCount: kind === 'config-error' ? 1 : 0,
61	    inputErrorCount: kind === 'input-error' ? 1 : 0,
62	    staleInputCount: kind === 'stale-input' ? 1 : 0,
63	    lastInputAt: occurredAt,
64	  };
65	}
66	
67	function frameHit(
68	  frame: ReceiveMatchedFrameSummary,
69	  source: ReceiveSourceSnapshot,
70	  occurredAt: string,
71	): ReceiveFrameStatisticsSnapshot {
72	  return {
73	    frameId: frame.frameId,
74	    frameName: frame.frameName,
75	    hitCount: 1,
76	    byteCount: frame.byteLength,
77	    lastMatchedAt: occurredAt,
78	    lastSourceId: source.sourceId,
79	  };
80	}
81	
82	function statsDelta(
83	  kind: ReceiveOutcomeKind,
84	  source: ReceiveSourceSnapshot | undefined,
85	  byteLength: number,
86	  occurredAt: string,
87	  matchedFrame?: ReceiveMatchedFrameSummary,
88	): ReceiveStatsDelta {
89	  return {
90	    batchCount: kind === 'input-error' ? 0 : 1,
91	    byteCount: kind === 'input-error' || kind === 'stale-input' ? 0 : byteLength,
92	    matchedCount: kind === 'matched' ? 1 : 0,
93	    unmatchedCount: kind === 'unmatched' ? 1 : 0,
94	    configErrorCount: kind === 'config-error' ? 1 : 0,
95	    parseErrorCount: kind === 'parse-error' ? 1 : 0,
96	    inputErrorCount: kind === 'input-error' ? 1 : 0,
97	    staleInputCount: kind === 'stale-input' ? 1 : 0,
98	    frameHits: matchedFrame && kind === 'matched' && source ? [frameHit(matchedFrame, source, occurredAt)] : [],
99	    sourceHits: source ? [sourceHit(source, kind, byteLength, occurredAt)] : [],
100	  };
101	}
102	
103	function outcome(
104	  id: string,
105	  kind: ReceiveOutcomeKind,
106	  processedAt: string,
107	  issues: readonly ReceiveIssue[],
108	  source: ReceiveSourceSnapshot | undefined,
109	  byteLength: number,
110	  input?: ReceiveInputBatch,
111	  matchedFrame?: ReceiveMatchedFrameSummary,
112	  fields: readonly ReceiveParsedFieldValue[] = [],
113	): ReceiveBatchOutcome {
114	  return {
115	    id,
116	    kind,
117	    processedAt,
118	    ...(input ? { input: cloneInputBatch(input) } : {}),
119	    ...(matchedFrame ? { matchedFrame: { ...matchedFrame } } : {}),
120	    fields: fields.map(cloneParsedField),
121	    issues: issues.map(cloneIssue),
122	    statsDelta: statsDelta(kind, source, byteLength, processedAt, matchedFrame),
123	  };
124	}
125	
126	function matchIssuesAreConfigErrors(issues: readonly ReceiveIssue[]): boolean {
127	  return issues.some(
128	    (issue) =>
129	      issue.severity === 'error' &&
130	      (issue.code === 'receive.frame.none' || issue.code === 'receive.frame.ruleMissing'),
131	  );
132	}
133	
134	export function createReceiveInputErrorOutcome(
135	  error: ReceiveInputError,
136	  processedAt: string,
137	): ReceiveBatchOutcome {
138	  const clonedError = cloneInputError(error);
139	  const issue = receiveIssue(
140	    `receive.input.${clonedError.kind}`,
141	    'input',
142	    clonedError.message,
143	  );
144	
145	  return outcome(
146	    clonedError.id,
147	    clonedError.kind === 'stale-reference' ? 'stale-input' : 'input-error',
148	    processedAt,
149	    [issue],
150	    clonedError.source,
151	    0,
152	  );
153	}
154	
155	export function createStaleReceiveInputOutcome(
156	  id: string,
157	  batch: ReceiveInputBatch,
158	  expectedReferenceVersion: number,
159	  processedAt: string,
160	): ReceiveBatchOutcome {
161	  return outcome(
162	    id,
163	    'stale-input',
164	    processedAt,
165	    [
166	      receiveIssue(
167	        'receive.input.staleReference',
168	        'batch.referenceVersion',
169	        `Input reference version does not match current receive reference version ${expectedReferenceVersion}.`,
170	      ),
171	    ],
172	    batch.source,
173	    batch.bytes.length,
174	    batch,
175	  );
176	}
177	
178	export function processReceiveBatch(input: ReceiveProcessInput): ReceiveBatchOutcome {
179	  const batch = cloneInputBatch(input.batch);
180	  const normalizedBytes = normalizeByteArray(batch.bytes, 'batch.bytes');
181	
182	  if (normalizedBytes.issues.length > 0) {
183	    return outcome(
184	      batch.id,
185	      'input-error',
186	      input.processedAt,
187	      normalizedBytes.issues,
188	      batch.source,
189	      0,
190	      batch,
191	    );
192	  }
193	
194	  const match = matchReceiveFrame(normalizedBytes.bytes, input.reference.frames);
195	  if (!match.matchedFrame) {
196	    const kind: ReceiveOutcomeKind = matchIssuesAreConfigErrors(match.issues)
197	      ? 'config-error'
198	      : 'unmatched';
199	
200	    return outcome(
201	      batch.id,
202	      kind,
203	      input.processedAt,
204	      match.issues,
205	      batch.source,
206	      normalizedBytes.bytes.length,
207	      batch,
208	    );
209	  }
210	
211	  const frame = cloneFrameReference(match.matchedFrame);
212	  const matchedFrame = matchedFrameSummary(
213	    frame.id,
214	    frame.name,
215	    normalizedBytes.bytes.length,
216	    frame.fields.length,
217	  );
218	  const parsed = parseReceiveFrameFields({
219	    frame,
220	    bytes: normalizedBytes.bytes,
221	  });
222	
223	  if (parsed.issues.some((issue) => issue.severity === 'error')) {
224	    return outcome(
225	      batch.id,
226	      'parse-error',
227	      input.processedAt,
228	      parsed.issues,
229	      batch.source,
230	      normalizedBytes.bytes.length,
231	      batch,
232	      matchedFrame,
233	      parsed.fields,
234	    );
235	  }
236	
237	  return outcome(
238	    batch.id,
239	    'matched',
240	    input.processedAt,
241	    parsed.issues,
242	    batch.source,
243	    normalizedBytes.bytes.length,
244	    batch,
245	    matchedFrame,
246	    parsed.fields,
247	  );
248	}
249	
250	export function createRecentInputHex(bytes: readonly number[]): string {
251	  return bytesToReadableHex(bytes);
252	}
253	
```

299	
300	  it('resolves intra-frame dependencies in correct order', () => {
301	    const cache = compileFrameExpressions([frameWithDependency]);
302	    const entry = cache.get(frameWithDependency.id)!;
303	
304	    // raw field = 5 → base = 5*2 = 10 → derived = 10+1 = 11
305	    const rawValues = new Map([
306	      ['field-raw', 5],
307	      ['field-base', 5],
308	      ['field-derived', 0],
309	    ]);
310	    const result = evaluateFrameExpressions({
311	      compiled: entry.compiled,
312	      frameId: frameWithDependency.id,
313	      currentFrameRawValues: rawValues,
314	      readModel: new Map(),
315	      globalParams: new Map(),
316	    });
317	
318	    expect(result.errors.size).toBe(0);
319	    expect(result.values.get('field-base')).toBe(10);
320	    expect(result.values.get('field-derived')).toBe(11);
321	  });
322	
323	  it('degrades gracefully when variable is missing', () => {
324	    const cache = compileFrameExpressions([frameWithExpression]);
325	    const entry = cache.get(frameWithExpression.id)!;
326	
327	    // No raw values provided → variable lookup fails
328	    const result = evaluateFrameExpressions({
329	      compiled: entry.compiled,
330	      frameId: frameWithExpression.id,
331	      currentFrameRawValues: new Map(),
332	      readModel: new Map(),
333	      globalParams: new Map(),
334	    });
335	
336	    expect(result.errors.has('field-speed-value')).toBe(true);
337	  });
338	
339	  it('resolves cross-frame values from read model', () => {
340	    const cache = compileFrameExpressions([frameWithDependency]);
341	    const entry = cache.get(frameWithDependency.id)!;
342	
343	    const readModel = new Map([
344	      ['frame-dep-test', new Map([['field-base', 42]])],
345	    ]);
346	    const rawValues = new Map([
347	      ['field-raw', 5],
348	      ['field-base', 5],
349	      ['field-derived', 0],
350	    ]);
351	    const result = evaluateFrameExpressions({
352	      compiled: entry.compiled,
353	      frameId: frameWithDependency.id,
354	      currentFrameRawValues: rawValues,
355	      readModel,
356	      globalParams: new Map(),
357	    });
358	
359	    // base = 5*2 = 10 (current_field resolves before read model)
360	    // derived = 10+1 = 11 (intra-frame resolved value takes priority over read model)
361	    expect(result.values.get('field-base')).toBe(10);
362	    expect(result.values.get('field-derived')).toBe(11);
363	  });
364	});
365	
366	describe('expression integration in processor', () => {
367	  it('marks fields expressionApplied=false when no expressions', () => {
368	    const outcome = processReceiveBatch({
369	      batch: batch([0xaa, 0x01, 0x00, 0x64, 0xff]),
370	      reference: reference([receiveTelemetryFrameFixture]),
371	      processedAt: '2026-05-08T00:00:03.000Z',
372	    });
373	
374	    expect(outcome.kind).toBe('matched');
375	    for (const field of outcome.fields) {

382	    const cache = compileFrameExpressions([frameWithExpression]);

390	
391	    expect(outcome.kind).toBe('matched');

393	    const speedCode = outcome.fields.find((f) => f.fieldId === 'field-speed-code')!;
394	    expect(speedCode.expressionApplied).toBe(false);

397	    expect(speedValue.expressionApplied).toBe(true);

402	  it('marks config-error when expression compile fails', () => {
403	    const cache = compileFrameExpressions([frameWithInvalidExpression]);

1	import type { ExpressionVariableDefinition, ReadonlyFrameAsset } from '@/features/frame';
2	import {
3	  compileConditional,
4	  defaultMathFunctions,
5	  evaluateConditional,
6	} from '@/shared/expression';
7	import type {
8	  CompiledConditional,
9	  FunctionTable,
10	  GroupEvalResult,
11	  VariableMap,
12	  VariableValue,
13	} from '@/shared/expression';
14	import type {
15	  ExpressionCompileCache,
16	  ExpressionEvalInput,
17	  FrameExpressionCompileResult,
18	} from './types';
19	
20	interface FieldWithExpression {
21	  readonly id: string;
22	  readonly name: string;
23	  readonly expressionConfig: {
24	    readonly expressions: ReadonlyArray<{ readonly condition: string; readonly expression: string }>;
25	    readonly variables: readonly ExpressionVariableDefinition[];
26	  };
27	}
28	
29	export function compileFrameExpressions(
30	  frames: readonly ReadonlyFrameAsset[],
31	  mathFunctions: FunctionTable = defaultMathFunctions,
32	): ExpressionCompileCache {
33	  const cache = new Map<string, FrameExpressionCompileResult>();
34	
35	  for (const frame of frames) {
36	    const fieldsWithExpr = frame.fields.filter(
37	      (f): f is typeof f & { expressionConfig: NonNullable<typeof f.expressionConfig> } =>
38	        !!f.expressionConfig && f.expressionConfig.expressions.length > 0,
39	    );
40	
41	    if (fieldsWithExpr.length === 0) continue;
42	
43	    const result = compileSingleFrame(frame.id, fieldsWithExpr, mathFunctions);
44	    cache.set(frame.id, result);
45	  }
46	
47	  return cache;
48	}
49	
50	function compileSingleFrame(
51	  frameId: string,
52	  fieldsWithExpr: readonly FieldWithExpression[],
53	  mathFunctions: FunctionTable,
54	): FrameExpressionCompileResult {
55	  const compiledFields = new Map<string, CompiledConditional>();
56	  const variablesMap = new Map<string, readonly ExpressionVariableDefinition[]>();
57	  const compileErrors: string[] = [];
58	
59	  for (const field of fieldsWithExpr) {
60	    const config = field.expressionConfig;
61	    const result = compileConditional(config.expressions, 0, mathFunctions);
62	
63	    if (!result.success) {
64	      compileErrors.push(`field "${field.name}": ${result.error}`);
65	      continue;
66	    }
67	
68	    compiledFields.set(field.id, result.compiled);
69	    variablesMap.set(field.id, config.variables);
70	  }
71	
72	  if (compileErrors.length > 0) {
73	    return { success: false, error: compileErrors.join('; ') };
74	  }
75	
76	  const orderResult = computeEvalOrder(fieldsWithExpr, frameId);
77	  if ('cycle' in orderResult) {
78	    return { success: false, error: `circular dependency: ${orderResult.cycle.join(', ')}` };
79	  }
80	
81	  return {
82	    success: true,
83	    compiled: {
84	      fields: compiledFields,
85	      variables: variablesMap,
86	      evalOrder: orderResult.order,
87	    },
88	  };
89	}
90	
91	function computeEvalOrder(
92	  fieldsWithExpr: readonly FieldWithExpression[],
93	  frameId: string,
94	): { readonly order: readonly string[] } | { readonly cycle: readonly string[] } {
95	  const fieldIds = new Set(fieldsWithExpr.map((f) => f.id));
96	  const deps = new Map<string, Set<string>>();
97	
98	  for (const field of fieldsWithExpr) {
99	    const fieldDeps = new Set<string>();
100	    for (const v of field.expressionConfig.variables) {
101	      if (
102	        v.sourceType === 'frame_field' &&
103	        v.frameId === frameId &&
104	        v.fieldId &&
105	        fieldIds.has(v.fieldId) &&
106	        v.fieldId !== field.id
107	      ) {
108	        fieldDeps.add(v.fieldId);
109	      }
110	    }
111	    deps.set(field.id, fieldDeps);
112	  }
113	
114	  const inDegree = new Map<string, number>();
115	  const adjList = new Map<string, Set<string>>();
116	
117	  for (const id of fieldIds) {
118	    inDegree.set(id, 0);
119	    adjList.set(id, new Set());
120	  }
121	
122	  for (const [id, idDeps] of deps) {
123	    for (const dep of idDeps) {
124	      adjList.get(dep)!.add(id);
125	      inDegree.set(id, (inDegree.get(id) ?? 0) + 1);
126	    }
127	  }
128	
129	  const queue: string[] = [];
130	  for (const [id, degree] of inDegree) {
131	    if (degree === 0) queue.push(id);
132	  }
133	
134	  const order: string[] = [];
135	  while (queue.length > 0) {
136	    const current = queue.shift()!;
137	    order.push(current);
138	    for (const neighbor of adjList.get(current) ?? []) {
139	      const newDegree = (inDegree.get(neighbor) ?? 1) - 1;
140	      inDegree.set(neighbor, newDegree);
141	      if (newDegree === 0) queue.push(neighbor);
142	    }
143	  }
144	
145	  if (order.length < fieldIds.size) {
146	    const cycleNodes = [...fieldIds].filter((id) => !order.includes(id));
147	    return { cycle: cycleNodes };
148	  }
149	
150	  return { order };
151	}
152	
153	export function evaluateFrameExpressions(input: ExpressionEvalInput): GroupEvalResult {
154	  const values = new Map<string, VariableValue>();
155	  const errors = new Map<string, string>();
156	  const resolvedValues = new Map<string, VariableValue>();
157	
158	  for (const fieldId of input.compiled.evalOrder) {
159	    const fieldCompiled = input.compiled.fields.get(fieldId);
160	    if (!fieldCompiled) {
161	      errors.set(fieldId, 'missing compiled expression');
162	      continue;
163	    }
164	
165	    const variableDefs = input.compiled.variables.get(fieldId) ?? [];
166	    const vars = buildVariableMap(
167	      variableDefs,
168	      input.currentFrameRawValues,
169	      resolvedValues,
170	      input.readModel,
171	      input.globalParams,
172	      input.frameId,
173	    );
174	
175	    const result = evaluateConditional(fieldCompiled, vars);
176	
177	    if (result.success) {
178	      values.set(fieldId, result.value);
179	      resolvedValues.set(fieldId, result.value);
180	    } else {
181	      errors.set(fieldId, result.error);
182	    }
183	  }
184	
185	  return { values, errors };
186	}
187	
188	function buildVariableMap(
189	  variableDefs: readonly ExpressionVariableDefinition[],
190	  currentFrameRawValues: ReadonlyMap<string, VariableValue>,
191	  currentFrameResolvedValues: ReadonlyMap<string, VariableValue>,
192	  readModel: ReadonlyMap<string, ReadonlyMap<string, VariableValue>>,
193	  globalParams: ReadonlyMap<string, VariableValue>,
194	  currentFrameId: string,
195	): VariableMap {
196	  const vars = new Map<string, VariableValue>();
197	
198	  for (const def of variableDefs) {
199	    let value: VariableValue | undefined;
200	
201	    switch (def.sourceType) {
202	      case 'current_field':
203	        if (def.fieldId) {
204	          value = currentFrameResolvedValues.get(def.fieldId)
205	            ?? currentFrameRawValues.get(def.fieldId);
206	        }
207	        break;
208	      case 'frame_field':
209	        if (def.frameId && def.fieldId) {
210	          if (def.frameId === currentFrameId) {
211	            value = currentFrameResolvedValues.get(def.fieldId)
212	              ?? currentFrameRawValues.get(def.fieldId);
213	          } else {
214	            value = readModel.get(def.frameId)?.get(def.fieldId);
215	          }
216	        }
217	        break;
218	      case 'global_stat':
219	        if (def.identifier) {
220	          value = globalParams.get(def.identifier);
221	        }
222	        break;
223	    }
224	
225	    if (value !== undefined) {
226	      vars.set(def.identifier, value);
227	    }
228	  }
229	
230	  return vars;
231	}
232	
```

expressionApplied: false })),\n    );\n  }\n\n  if (compileResult?.success && compileResult.compiled) {\n    const rawValues = new Map(\n      parsed.fields.map((f) => [f.fieldId, f.value as number | string]),\n    );\n    const evalResult = evaluateFrameExpressions({\n      compiled: compileResult.compiled,\n      frameId: frame.id,\n      currentFrameRawValues: rawValues,\n      readModel: new Map(),\n      globalParams: new Map(),\n    });\n\n    const updatedFields = parsed.fields.map((f) => {\n      if (!compileResult.compiled!.fields.has(f.fieldId)) {\n        return { ...f, expressionApplied: false };\n      }\n\n      const error = evalResult.errors.get(f.fieldId);\n      if (error) {\n        return { ...f, expressionApplied: true, expressionError: error };\n      }\n\n      const evalValue = evalResult.values.get(f.fieldId);\n      if (evalValue !== undefined) {\n        return {\n          ...f,\n          expressionApplied: true,\n          value: evalValue as ReceiveParsedFieldPrimitive,\n          displayValue: String(evalValue),\n        };\n      }\n\n      return { ...f, expressionApplied: false };\n    });\n\n    const evalIssues: ReceiveIssue[] = [];\n    for (const [fieldId, error] of evalResult.errors) {\n      evalIssues.push(\n        receiveIssue('receive.expression.evalFailed', `field.${fieldId}`, error),\n      );\n    }\n\n    return outcome(\n      batch.id,\n      'matched',\n      input.processedAt,\n      [...parsed.issues, ...evalIssues],\n      batch.source,\n      normalizedBytes.bytes.length,\n      batch,\n      matchedFrame,\n      updatedFields,\n    );\n  }\n\n  // No expression cache or no compile result for this frame\n  return outcome(\n    batch.id,\n    'matched',\n    input.processedAt,\n    parsed.issues,\n    batch.source,\n    normalizedBytes.bytes.length,\n    batch,\n    matchedFrame,\n    parsed.fields.map((f) => ({ ...f, expressionApplied: false })),\n  );\n}"

34	async function settle(service: TaskService, instanceId: string, timeoutMs = 2000): Promise<void> {
35	  const deadline = Date.now() + timeoutMs;
36	  while (Date.now() < deadline) {
37	    const inst = service.getInstance(instanceId);
38	    if (!inst || inst.lifecycle === 'completed' || inst.lifecycle === 'stopped' || inst.lifecycle === 'failed' || inst.lifecycle === 'paused') {
39	      return;
40	    }
41	    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 5));
42	  }
43	}

9	export type ReadonlyTaskInstanceState = ReadonlyDeep<TaskInstanceState>;
10	
11	export function selectTaskInstances(snapshot: TaskStateSnapshot): readonly ReadonlyTaskInstanceState[] {
12	  return [...snapshot.instances] as readonly ReadonlyTaskInstanceState[];
13	}
14	
15	export function selectTaskInstance(
16	  snapshot: TaskStateSnapshot,
17	  instanceId: string,
18	): ReadonlyTaskInstanceState | undefined {
19	  const instance = snapshot.instances.find((i) => i.instanceId === instanceId);
20	  return instance ? structuredClone(instance) as ReadonlyTaskInstanceState : undefined;
21	}
22	
23	export function selectTaskProgress(
24	  snapshot: TaskStateSnapshot,
25	  instanceId: string,
26	): TaskProgress | undefined {
27	  const instance = snapshot.instances.find((i) => i.instanceId === instanceId);
28	  return instance ? calculateProgress(instance) : undefined;
29	}
30	
31	const TERMINATED_LIFECYCLES = new Set(['completed', 'failed', 'stopped']);
32	
33	export function selectTaskHistory(snapshot: TaskStateSnapshot): readonly ReadonlyTaskInstanceState[] {
34	  const historyInstances = snapshot.instances.filter(
35	    (i) => TERMINATED_LIFECYCLES.has(i.lifecycle),
36	  );
37	  const historyIds = new Set(snapshot.history.map((i) => i.instanceId));
38	  const merged = [...snapshot.history, ...historyInstances.filter((i) => !historyIds.has(i.instanceId))];
39	  return merged as readonly ReadonlyTaskInstanceState[];
40	}
41	
42	export function selectTaskStatistics(snapshot: TaskStateSnapshot): Readonly<TaskStatisticsSnapshot> {
43	  return { ...snapshot.statistics };
44	}
45	
46	export function selectActiveInstances(snapshot: TaskStateSnapshot): readonly ReadonlyTaskInstanceState[] {
47	  return snapshot.instances.filter(
48	    (i) => i.lifecycle === 'created' || i.lifecycle === 'running' || i.lifecycle === 'paused',
49	  ) as readonly ReadonlyTaskInstanceState[];
50	}
51	
52	export interface TaskUiSnapshot {
53	  readonly instances: readonly ReadonlyTaskInstanceState[];
54	  readonly history: readonly ReadonlyTaskInstanceState[];
55	  readonly statistics: Readonly<TaskStatisticsSnapshot>;
56	}
57	
58	export function selectTaskSnapshot(snapshot: TaskStateSnapshot): TaskUiSnapshot {
59	  return {
60	    instances: [...snapshot.instances] as readonly ReadonlyTaskInstanceState[],
61	    history: [...snapshot.history] as readonly ReadonlyTaskInstanceState[],
62	    statistics: { ...snapshot.statistics },

20	export interface CommandIngressServiceOptions {
21	  readonly globalConfig: ScoeProtocolAdapterOptions['globalConfig'];
22	  readonly commandConfigs: ScoeProtocolAdapterOptions['commandConfigs'];
23	  readonly satelliteConfigs: readonly SatelliteConfig[];
24	  readonly taskService: TaskService;
25	  readonly sendService: SendService;
26	  readonly frameReader: FrameAssetReader;
27	  readonly connectionService: ConnectionService;
28	  readonly connectionSnapshot: () => ConnectionStateSnapshot;
29	  readonly receiveSnapshot: () => unknown;

31	  readonly stateReader: CommandIngressStateReader;
32	  readonly stateWriter: CommandIngressStateWriter;
33	}

35	export interface CommandIngressService {
36	  readonly adapter: ProtocolAdapter;
37	  loadSatellite(satelliteId: string): Promise<CommandResult>;
38	  unloadSatellite(): Promise<CommandResult>;
39	  getScoeStatistics(): ScoeStatisticsSnapshot;

41	  getLoadedSatelliteId(): string;
42	  isScoeFramesLoaded(): boolean;
43	  dispose(): void;
44	}
45	
46	export function createCommandIngressService(
47	  options: CommandIngressServiceOptions,
48	): CommandIngressService {
49	  const {
50	    satelliteConfigs,
51	    taskService,
52	    sendService,
53	    frameReader,
54	    connectionService,
55	    connectionSnapshot,
56	    receiveSnapshot,
57	    platformFileReader,
58	    stateReader,
59	    stateWriter,
60	  } = options;
61	
62	  // Tracking: connectionId → taskInstanceIds
63	  const trackingMap = new Map<string, Set<string>>();
64	
65	  // Handler map
66	  const handlerMap = new Map<ScoeCommandFunction, CommandHandler>([
67	    ['load_satellite_id', createHandleLoadSatelliteId(satelliteConfigs)],
68	    ['unload_satellite_id', handleUnloadSatelliteId],
69	    ['health_check', handleHealthCheck],
70	    ['link_check', handleLinkCheck],
71	    ['send_frame', handleSendFrame],
72	    ['read_file_and_send', handleReadFileAndSend],
73	  ]);
74	
75	  const ctx: CommandContext = {
76	    taskService,
77	    sendService,
78	    frameReader,
79	    connectionService,
80	    connectionSnapshot,
81	    receiveSnapshot,
82	    platformFileReader,
83	    stateReader,
84	    stateWriter,
85	  };
86	
87	  // onCommand: dispatch parsed commands, track tasks, monitor settlement
88	  async function onCommand(parsed: Parameters<typeof dispatchCommand>[0]): Promise<void> {
89	    stateWriter.updateStatus({ lastCommandCode: parsed.commandCode });
90	
91	    const result = await dispatchCommand(parsed, handlerMap, ctx);
92	
93	    if (result.taskId) {
94	      trackTask(parsed.connectionId, result.taskId, parsed);
95	    }
96	
97	    cleanupDisconnectedConnections();
98	  }
99	
100	  // Create protocol adapter with onCommand dispatch
101	  const adapter = createScoeProtocolAdapter({
102	    globalConfig: options.globalConfig,
103	    commandConfigs: options.commandConfigs,
104	    stateReader,
105	    onCommand,
106	  });
107	
108	  // Start status timer (inlined from status-timer.ts)
109	  const timerHandle = setInterval(() => {
110	    stateWriter.tickSecond();
111	  }, 1000);
112	
113	  function trackTask(
114	    connectionId: string,
115	    taskId: string,
116	    command: Parameters<typeof dispatchCommand>[0],
117	  ): void {
118	    let set = trackingMap.get(connectionId);
119	    if (!set) {
120	      set = new Set();
121	      trackingMap.set(connectionId, set);
122	    }
123	    set.add(taskId);
124	
125	    // Single consolidated settlement monitor: cleanup tracking + ack frame + counters
126	    taskService.onSettled(taskId)
127	      .then(async () => {
128	        const inst = taskService.getInstance(taskId);
129	        if (inst?.lifecycle === 'completed') {
130	          try {
131	            await sendAckFrame(command, ctx);
132	          } catch {
133	            // Ack frame send failure — don't crash, just count as error
134	          }
135	          stateWriter.incrementSuccessCount();
136	        } else {
137	          stateWriter.incrementErrorCount(inst?.error ?? 'task failed');
138	        }
139	
140	        // Re-lookup Set from trackingMap (avoids stale closure reference)
141	        const current = trackingMap.get(connectionId);
142	        if (!current) return;
143	        current.delete(taskId);
144	        if (current.size === 0) {
145	          trackingMap.delete(connectionId);
146	        }
147	      })
148	      .catch((err) => {
149	        stateWriter.incrementErrorCount(
150	          err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'settlement tracking failed',
151	        );
152	        // Still cleanup tracking
153	        const current = trackingMap.get(connectionId);
154	        if (current) {
155	          current.delete(taskId);
156	          if (current.size === 0) trackingMap.delete(connectionId);
157	        }
158	      });
159	  }
160	
161	  function cleanupDisconnectedConnections(): void {
162	    for (const [connId, taskIds] of trackingMap) {
163	      const fact = connectionService.getConnectionFact(connId);
164	      if (!fact || fact.lifecycle === 'disconnected') {
165	        for (const taskId of taskIds) {
166	          try {
167	            taskService.stopTask(taskId);
168	          } catch {
169	            // Task may already be settled
170	          }
171	        }
172	        trackingMap.delete(connId);
173	      }
174	    }
175	  }
176	
177	  function dispose(): void {
178	    clearInterval(timerHandle);
179	    for (const [, taskIds] of trackingMap) {
180	      for (const taskId of taskIds) {
181	        try {
182	          taskService.stopTask(taskId);
183	        } catch {
184	          // Ignore errors during cleanup
185	        }
186	      }
187	    }
188	    trackingMap.clear();
189	  }
190	
191	  // CI-R2: delegate loadSatellite to handler logic
192	  const loadHandler = createHandleLoadSatelliteId(satelliteConfigs);
193	  async function loadSatellite(satelliteId: string): Promise<CommandResult> {
194	    if (stateReader.loadedSatelliteId !== '') {
195	      return { success: false, error: 'Satellite already loaded' };
196	    }
197	    const satConfig = satelliteConfigs.find((s) => s.satelliteId === satelliteId);
198	    if (!satConfig) {
199	      return { success: false, error: `Unknown satellite ID: ${satelliteId}` };
200	    }
201	    stateWriter.setLoaded(satelliteId, satConfig.commandConfigs);
202	    return { success: true };
203	  }
204	
205	  async function unloadSatellite(): Promise<CommandResult> {
206	    if (!stateReader.scoeFramesLoaded) {
207	      return { success: false, error: 'No satellite loaded' };
208	    }
209	    stateWriter.resetRuntimeState();
210	    return { success: true };
211	  }
212	
213	  // CI-R5: Reader methods replacing deleted selectors
214	  function getScoeStatistics(): ScoeStatisticsSnapshot {
215	    const s = stateReader.getSnapshot();
216	    return {
217	      commandReceiveCount: s.commandReceiveCount,
218	      commandSuccessCount: s.commandSuccessCount,
219	      commandErrorCount: s.commandErrorCount,
220	      runtimeSeconds: s.runtimeSeconds,
221	      satelliteIdRuntimeSeconds: s.satelliteIdRuntimeSeconds,
222	      lastErrorReason: s.lastErrorReason,
223	    };
224	  }
225	
226	  function getScoeRuntimeStatus(): ScoeRuntimeStatus {
227	    const s = stateReader.getSnapshot();
228	    return {
229	      loadedSatelliteId: s.loadedSatelliteId,
230	      scoeFramesLoaded: s.scoeFramesLoaded,
231	      healthStatus: s.healthStatus,
232	      linkTestResult: s.linkTestResult,
233	      lastCommandCode: s.lastCommandCode,
234	      receiveCommandSuccess: s.receiveCommandSuccess,
235	    };
236	  }
237	
238	  function getLoadedSatelliteId(): string {
239	    return stateReader.loadedSatelliteId;
240	  }
241	
242	  function isScoeFramesLoaded(): boolean {
243	    return stateReader.scoeFramesLoaded;
244	  }
245	
246	  return {
247	    adapter,
248	    loadSatellite,
249	    unloadSatellite,
250	    getScoeStatistics,
251	    getScoeRuntimeStatus,
252	    getLoadedSatelliteId,
253	    isScoeFramesLoaded,
254	    dispose,
255	  };
256	}
257

{
  "file_path": "/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/command-ingress/core/command-log.ts",
  "content": "import { nanoid } from 'nanoid';\n\nexport interface CommandLogEntry {\n  readonly id: string;\n  readonly timestamp: string;\n  readonly commandCode: number;\n  readonly result: 'success' | 'error' | 'pending';\n  readonly durationMs?: number;\n  readonly error?: string;\n}\n\nexport interface CommandLogRecorder {\n  record(entry: Omit<CommandLogEntry, 'id'>): CommandLogEntry;\n  complete(id: string, patch: Partial<Pick<CommandLogEntry, 'result' | 'durationMs' | 'error'>>): void;\n  getAll(): readonly CommandLogEntry[];\n  clear(): void;\n}\n\nexport function createCommandLogRecorder(maxEntries = 1000): CommandLogRecorder {\n  const entries: CommandLogEntry[] = [];\n\n  return {\n    record(entry) {\n      const full: CommandLogEntry = { ...entry, id: nanoid() };\n      entries.push(full);\n      if (entries.length > maxEntries) entries.shift();\n      return full;\n    },\n\n    complete(id, patch) {\n      const idx = entries.findIndex((e) => e.id === id);\n      if (idx === -1) return;\n      entries[idx] = { ...entries[idx], ...patch };\n    },\n\n    getAll() {\n      return [...entries];\n    },\n\n    clear() {\n      entries.length = 0;\n    },\n  };\n}\n"
}

  "old_string": "  // onCommand: dispatch parsed commands, track tasks, monitor settlement\n  async function onCommand(parsed: Parameters<typeof dispatchCommand>[0]): Promise<void> {\n    stateWriter.updateStatus({ lastCommandCode: parsed.commandCode });\n\n    const result = await dispatchCommand(parsed, handlerMap, ctx);\n\n    if (result.taskId) {\n      trackTask(parsed.connectionId, result.taskId, parsed);\n    }\n\n    cleanupDisconnectedConnections();\n  }",
  "new_string": "  // onCommand: dispatch parsed commands, track tasks, monitor settlement\n  async function onCommand(parsed: Parameters<typeof dispatchCommand>[0]): Promise<void> {\n    stateWriter.updateStatus({ lastCommandCode: parsed.commandCode });\n\n    const logEntry = logRecorder.record({\n      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),\n      commandCode: parsed.commandCode,\n      result: 'pending',\n    });\n\n    const result = await dispatchCommand(parsed, handlerMap, ctx);\n\n    if (result.taskId) {\n      trackTask(parsed.connectionId, result.taskId, parsed, logEntry.id);\n    } else {\n      logRecorder.complete(logEntry.id, {\n        result: result.success ? 'success' : 'error',\n        error: result.error,\n      });\n    }\n\n    cleanupDisconnectedConnections();\n  }"
}

'task failed');\n          logRecorder.complete(logEntryId, {\n            result: 'error',\n            error: inst?.error ?? 'task failed',\n          });\n        }"

  "old_string": "  return {\n    adapter,\n    loadSatellite,\n    unloadSatellite,\n    getScoeStatistics,\n    getScoeRuntimeStatus,\n    getLoadedSatelliteId,\n    isScoeFramesLoaded,\n    dispose,\n  };\n}",
  "new_string": "  function getCommandLog(): readonly CommandLogEntry[] {\n    return logRecorder.getAll();\n  }\n\n  function clearCommandLog(): void {\n    logRecorder.clear();\n  }\n\n  function getTestDataRecorder(): DataRecorder {\n    return testDataRecorder;\n  }\n\n  async function sendTestData(hex: string, connectionId: string): Promise<void> {\n    const bytes = hexToBytes(hex);\n    await connectionService.write({ connectionId, bytes });\n  }\n\n  return {\n    adapter,\n    loadSatellite,\n    unloadSatellite,\n    getScoeStatistics,\n    getScoeRuntimeStatus,\n    getLoadedSatelliteId,\n    isScoeFramesLoaded,\n    getCommandLog,\n    clearCommandLog,\n    getTestDataRecorder,\n    sendTestData,\n    dispose,\n  };\n}\n\nfunction hexToBytes(hex: string): number[] {\n  const cleaned = hex.replace(/\\s/g, '');\n  if (cleaned.length % 2 !== 0) {\n    throw new Error('Hex string must have even length');\n  }\n  const bytes: number[] = [];\n  for (let i = 0; i < cleaned.length; i += 2) {\n    bytes.push(parseInt(cleaned.substring(i, i + 2), 16));\n  }\n  return bytes;\n}"
}

1	export type HighlightSeverity = 'info' | 'warning' | 'error';
2	
3	export interface HighlightRule {
4	  readonly id: string;
5	  readonly match: (value: unknown) => boolean;
6	  readonly severity: HighlightSeverity;
7	}
8	
9	export interface Highlight {
10	  readonly ruleId: string;
11	  readonly severity: HighlightSeverity;
12	  readonly matched: true;
13	}
14	
15	export function calculateHighlights(
16	  data: Record<string, unknown>,
17	  rules: readonly HighlightRule[],
18	): Highlight[] {
19	  const results: Highlight[] = [];
20	
21	  for (const rule of rules) {
22	    for (const value of Object.values(data)) {
23	      if (rule.match(value)) {
24	        results.push({ ruleId: rule.id, severity: rule.severity, matched: true });
25	        break;
26	      }
27	    }
28	  }
29	
30	  return results;
31	}
32

1	import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
2	import { calculateHighlights } from '../core/highlight';
3	import type { HighlightRule } from '../core/highlight';
4	
5	describe('calculateHighlights', () => {
6	  const rules: HighlightRule[] = [
7	    {
8	      id: 'high-error-count',
9	      match: (v) => typeof v === 'number' && v > 10,
10	      severity: 'error',
11	    },
12	    {
13	      id: 'stale-runtime',
14	      match: (v) => typeof v === 'number' && v > 3600,
15	      severity: 'warning',
16	    },
17	    {
18	      id: 'has-satellite',
19	      match: (v) => typeof v === 'string' && v.length > 0,
20	      severity: 'info',
21	    },
22	  ];
23	
24	  it('returns empty array when no rules match', () => {
25	    const result = calculateHighlights({ count: 1, elapsed: 5 }, rules);
26	    expect(result).toHaveLength(0);
27	  });
28	
29	  it('matches single rule', () => {
30	    const result = calculateHighlights({ errorCount: 15 }, rules);
31	    expect(result).toHaveLength(1);
32	    expect(result[0].ruleId).toBe('high-error-count');
33	    expect(result[0].severity).toBe('error');
34	  });
35	
36	  it('matches multiple rules', () => {
37	    const result = calculateHighlights(
38	      { errorCount: 20, runtimeSeconds: 5000, satelliteId: 'SAT-01' },
39	      rules,
40	    );
41	    expect(result).toHaveLength(3);
42	    expect(result.map((h) => h.ruleId)).toEqual([
43	      'high-error-count',
44	      'stale-runtime',
45	      'has-satellite',
46	    ]);
47	  });
48	
49	  it('each rule matches at most once even if multiple values match', () => {
50	    const result = calculateHighlights(
51	      { a: 20, b: 30, c: 40 },
52	      rules,
53	    );
54	    expect(result).toHaveLength(1);
55	    expect(result[0].ruleId).toBe('high-error-count');
56	  });
57	
58	  it('returns empty for empty data', () => {
59	    const result = calculateHighlights({}, rules);
60	    expect(result).toHaveLength(0);
61	  });
62	
63	  it('returns empty for empty rules', () => {
64	    const result = calculateHighlights({ errorCount: 99 }, []);
65	    expect(result).toHaveLength(0);
66	  });
67	});
68

{
  "file_path": "/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/command-ingress/core/highlight.ts",
  "content": "export type HighlightSeverity = 'info' | 'warning' | 'error';\n\n/**\n * Data-driven highlight rule — fully serializable (no functions).\n */\nexport interface HighlightRuleConfig {\n  readonly id: string;\n  readonly offset: number;\n  readonly length: number;\n  readonly pattern?: string;\n  readonly severity: HighlightSeverity;\n}\n\n/**\n * @deprecated Use HighlightRuleConfig instead. The `match` function prevents serialization.\n */\nexport interface HighlightRule {\n  readonly id: string;\n  match: (value: unknown) => boolean;\n  readonly severity: HighlightSeverity;\n}\n\nexport interface Highlight {\n  readonly ruleId: string;\n  readonly severity: HighlightSeverity;\n  readonly matched: true;\n}\n\nexport function calculateHighlights(\n  data: Uint8Array,\n  rules: readonly HighlightRuleConfig[],\n): Highlight[];\n\n/** @deprecated Use the Uint8Array + HighlightRuleConfig overload */\nexport function calculateHighlights(\n  data: Record<string, unknown>,\n  rules: readonly HighlightRule[],\n): Highlight[];\n\nexport function calculateHighlights(\n  data: Uint8Array | Record<string, unknown>,\n  rules: readonly (HighlightRuleConfig | HighlightRule)[],\n): Highlight[] {\n  const results: Highlight[] = [];\n\n  if (data instanceof Uint8Array) {\n    for (const rule of rules) {\n      if (!('offset' in rule)) continue;\n      const cfg = rule as HighlightRuleConfig;\n      if (cfg.offset + cfg.length > data.length) continue;\n\n      if (!cfg.pattern) {\n        results.push({ ruleId: cfg.id, severity: cfg.severity, matched: true });\n        continue;\n      }\n\n      const patternBytes = hexToBytes(cfg.pattern);\n      let match = true;\n      for (let i = 0; i < patternBytes.length && i < cfg.length; i++) {\n        if (data[cfg.offset + i] !== patternBytes[i]) {\n          match = false;\n          break;\n        }\n      }\n      if (match) {\n        results.push({ ruleId: cfg.id, severity: cfg.severity, matched: true });\n      }\n    }\n  } else {\n    for (const rule of rules) {\n      if (!('match' in rule)) continue;\n      const legacy = rule as HighlightRule;\n      for (const value of Object.values(data)) {\n        if (legacy.match(value)) {\n          results.push({ ruleId: legacy.id, severity: legacy.severity, matched: true });\n          break;\n        }\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  return results;\n}\n\nfunction hexToBytes(hex: string): number[] {\n  const cleaned = hex.replace(/\\s/g, '');\n  const bytes: number[] = [];\n  for (let i = 0; i < cleaned.length; i += 2) {\n    bytes.push(parseInt(cleaned.substring(i, i + 2), 16));\n  }\n  return bytes;\n}\n"
}

  "content": "import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';\nimport { calculateHighlights } from '../core/highlight';\nimport type { HighlightRuleConfig } from '../core/highlight';\n\ndescribe('calculateHighlights with HighlightRuleConfig', () => {\n  const rules: HighlightRuleConfig[] = [\n    { id: 'header-match', offset: 0, length: 2, pattern: 'BBAA', severity: 'info' },\n    { id: 'any-range', offset: 4, length: 3, severity: 'warning' },\n    { id: 'out-of-bounds', offset: 100, length: 2, severity: 'error' },\n  ];\n\n  it('matches pattern at offset', () => {\n    const data = new Uint8Array([0xBB, 0xAA, 0x00, 0x00, 0x01, 0x02, 0x03]);\n    const result = calculateHighlights(data, rules);\n    expect(result).toHaveLength(2);\n    expect(result.map((h) => h.ruleId)).toContain('header-match');\n    expect(result.map((h) => h.ruleId)).toContain('any-range');\n  });\n\n  it('skips rule when pattern does not match', () => {\n    const data = new Uint8Array([0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x01, 0x02, 0x03]);\n    const result = calculateHighlights(data, rules);\n    expect(result).toHaveLength(1);\n    expect(result[0]?.ruleId).toBe('any-range');\n  });\n\n  it('skips out-of-bounds rules', () => {\n    const data = new Uint8Array([0xBB, 0xAA, 0x00, 0x00, 0x01, 0x02, 0x03]);\n    const result = calculateHighlights(data, rules);\n    const oob = result.find((h) => h.ruleId === 'out-of-bounds');\n    expect(oob).toBeUndefined();\n  });\n\n  it('returns empty for empty rules', () => {\n    const data = new Uint8Array([0x01, 0x02]);\n    expect(calculateHighlights(data, [])).toHaveLength(0);\n  });\n});\n"
}

1	import type { DisplayService, DisplayFieldMaterial } from '@/features/display';
2	import type { ReceiveBatchOutcome } from '@/features/receive';
3	
4	export function fanOutToDisplay(
5	  service: DisplayService,
6	  outcomes: readonly ReceiveBatchOutcome[],
7	): number {
8	  const fields: DisplayFieldMaterial[] = [];
9	
10	  for (const outcome of outcomes) {
11	    if (outcome.kind !== 'matched') continue;
12	    if (outcome.fields.length === 0) continue;
13	
14	    for (const f of outcome.fields) {
15	      fields.push({
16	        frameId: f.frameId,
17	        fieldId: f.fieldId,
18	        fieldName: f.fieldName,
19	        value: f.value,
20	        displayValue: f.displayValue,
21	        updatedAt: outcome.processedAt,
22	      });
23	    }
24	  }
25	
26	  if (fields.length === 0) return 0;
27	
28	  const result = service.ingestSourceMaterial({ fields });
29	  return result.ok ? fields.length : 0;
30	}
31	
```

21	boundary_guards:
22	  - "只实现 connection transport config、runtime connection facts、target availability、transport-level lifecycle/error/event。"
23	  - "不实现 serial/TCP client/TCP server/UDP 真实平台链路，除非后续 implementation 另行进入实现阶段。"
24	  - "不冻结 preload/main/renderer API schema、channel、method、DTO、字段、错误码或枚举。"
25	  - "不实现 frame parsing、receive matching、send task state、SCOE semantics、result、report、northbound 内部语义。"
26	  - "main 只承载平台 transport 资源、生命周期和必要 buffer/batch/queue/backpressure，不解释业务语义。"
27	  - "renderer 不直接访问 Node、Electron、ipcRenderer、fs、path、net、serialport 或旧 window.electron。"
28	  - "serial/network target 不等同 northbound deviceId、业务设备身份、task、case 或 report delivery target。"
29	  - "旧 src/src-electron/public 只作为 evidence、fixture 或 oracle 输入。"
30	  - "不读取或修改前端自动生成 types 文件。"

1	import type { TaskInstanceState } from '@/features/task';
2	import type { CaseVerdict } from '@/features/result';
3	import { isStepFailed } from '@/features/result';
4	import type { ReportJson, ReportStepEntry } from './types';
5	
6	export function mapSteps(instance: TaskInstanceState): readonly ReportStepEntry[] {
7	  return instance.stepResults.map((step) => ({
8	    index: step.stepIndex,
9	    kind: step.kind,
10	    passed: !isStepFailed(step),
11	  }));
12	}
13	
14	export function generateReport(instance: TaskInstanceState, verdict: CaseVerdict): ReportJson {
15	  return {
16	    reportId: `report-${verdict.instanceId}`,
17	    taskDefinitionId: verdict.taskDefinitionId,
18	    taskDefinitionName: instance.definitionRef.name,
19	    verdict: verdict.verdict,
20	    startedAt: verdict.startedAt,
21	    finishedAt: verdict.finishedAt,
22	    steps: mapSteps(instance),
23	  };
24	}
