1	import type { TaskInstanceState, TaskStepResult } from '@/features/task';
2	import type { CaseVerdictKind } from './types';
3	
4	export function isStepFailed(step: TaskStepResult): boolean {
5	  switch (step.kind) {
6	    case 'send':
7	      return step.sendResult.kind !== 'sent';
8	    case 'wait-condition':
9	      return !step.matched;
10	    case 'delay':
11	      return !step.completed;
12	  }
13	}
14	
15	export function judgeCaseVerdict(instance: TaskInstanceState): CaseVerdictKind {
16	  if (instance.lifecycle === 'stopped') return 'stopped';
17	  if (instance.lifecycle === 'failed') return 'failed';
18	  const hasFailure = instance.stepResults.some(isStepFailed);
19	  return hasFailure ? 'failed' : 'passed';
20	}
21	
```

> AGENT

问题是 retry policy 测试在全局跑时超时（2s settle 不够）。实际上这个测试用了 `schedule: { kind: 'immediate' }` + `retryTwice`，理论上应该很快完成。但多个测试文件跑时，可能有定时器/微任务干扰。

让我增大 settle 超时并确认这不是根本问题——先看看是否是 task-real agent 的循环引擎实现有 bug。

21	  now: () => string;
22	}

29	}

34	  instanceId: string,
35	  signal: Promise<void>,
36	): ScheduleDriverAdapter {
37	  switch (schedule.kind) {
38	    case 'immediate':
39	      return { waitForNext: async () => true, dispose: () => {} };

40	
41	    case 'timer':
42	      return createTimerDriver(schedule.intervalMs);
43	
44	    case 'event':
45	      return createEventDriver(schedule, ctx, instanceId, signal);
46	  }
47	}
48	
49	function createTimerDriver(intervalMs: number): ScheduleDriverAdapter {
50	  let firstCall = true;
51	
52	  return {
53	    async waitForNext(_id, _iter, signal) {
54	      if (firstCall) { firstCall = false; return true; }
55	
56	      const delay = sleep(intervalMs);
57	      const race = await Promise.race([
58	        delay.promise.then(() => true),
59	        signal.then(() => false),
60	      ]);
61	      if (!race) delay.cancel();
62	      return race;
63	    },
64	    dispose() {},
65	  };
66	}
67	
68	function createEventDriver(
69	  schedule: Extract<ScheduleDriver, { kind: 'event' }>,
70	  ctx: TaskExecutionContext,
71	  _instanceId: string,
72	  signal: Promise<void>,
73	): ScheduleDriverAdapter {
74	  const { conditions, cooldownMs = 0 } = schedule;
75	  let lastTriggerTime = 0;
76	  let notifyResolve: ((value: boolean) => void) | null = null;
77	  let disposed = false;
78	
79	  const group = ctx.conditionRegistry.registerGroup(conditions, () => {
80	    if (disposed) return;
81	    const now = Date.now();
82	    if (cooldownMs > 0 && lastTriggerTime > 0 && now - lastTriggerTime < cooldownMs) return;
83	    lastTriggerTime = now;
84	    if (notifyResolve) { notifyResolve(true); notifyResolve = null; }
85	  });
86	
87	  return {
88	    async waitForNext() {
89	      if (disposed) return false;
90	      return Promise.race([
91	        new Promise<boolean>((resolve) => { notifyResolve = resolve; }),
92	        signal.then(() => false),
93	      ]);
94	    },
95	    dispose() {
96	      disposed = true;
97	      ctx.conditionRegistry.unregisterGroup(group);
98	      if (notifyResolve) { notifyResolve(false); notifyResolve = null; }
99	    },
100	  };
101	}
102	
103	// --- StopGuard ---
104	
105	interface StopGuard {
106	  shouldStop(instanceId: string, iteration: number): boolean;
107	  checkExitCondition(): boolean;
108	}
109	
110	function createStopGuard(stop: ResolvedStopCondition, ctx: TaskExecutionContext): StopGuard {
111	  const startTime = Date.now();
112	
113	  return {
114	    shouldStop(instanceId, iteration) {
115	      const inst = ctx.state.getInstance(instanceId);
116	      if (!inst || inst.lifecycle !== 'running') return true;
117	      if (stop.maxIterations !== undefined && iteration >= stop.maxIterations) return true;
118	      if (stop.maxDurationMs !== undefined && Date.now() - startTime >= stop.maxDurationMs) return true;
119	      return false;
120	    },
121	
122	    checkExitCondition() {
123	      if (!stop.exitCondition) return false;
124	      if (!ctx.fieldValueProvider) return false;
125	      return evaluateConditionGroup(stop.exitCondition, ctx.fieldValueProvider());
126	    },
127	  };
128	}
129	
130	// --- fieldVariations injection ---
131	
132	function resolveFieldValues(
133	  baseValues: Readonly<Record<string, string | number | boolean>> | undefined,
134	  fieldVariations: readonly FieldVariation[] | undefined,
135	  iteration: number,
136	): Readonly<Record<string, string | number | boolean>> {
137	  if (!fieldVariations || fieldVariations.length === 0) return baseValues ?? {};
138	
139	  const result: Record<string, string | number | boolean> = { ...(baseValues ?? {}) };

148	// --- executeRepeatableSend ---
149	
150	async function executeRepeatableSend(
151	  ctx: TaskExecutionContext,
152	  instanceId: string,
153	  step: Extract<TaskDefinition['steps'][number], { kind: 'send' }>,
154	  iteration: number,
155	  stepIndex: number,
156	  definition: TaskDefinition,
157	  signal: Promise<void>,
158	): Promise<boolean> {
159	  const { repeat } = step.config;
160	  if (!repeat) {
161	    const result = await ctx.stepExecutors.executeSendStep(instanceId, step, iteration, stepIndex, definition);
162	    ctx.state.addStepResult(instanceId, result);
163	    return result.sendResult.kind === 'sent';
164	  }
165	
166	  let count = 0;
167	  const maxCount = repeat.maxCount ?? Infinity;
168	
169	  while (count < maxCount) {
170	    const result = await ctx.stepExecutors.executeSendStep(instanceId, step, iteration, stepIndex, definition);
171	    ctx.state.addStepResult(instanceId, result);
172	
173	    if (result.sendResult.kind !== 'sent') return false;
174	
175	    count++;
176	
177	    if (repeat.until && ctx.fieldValueProvider) {
178	      if (evaluateConditionGroup(repeat.until, ctx.fieldValueProvider())) break;
179	    }
180	
181	    if (count < maxCount) {
182	      const delay = sleep(repeat.intervalMs);
183	      const race = await Promise.race([delay.promise.then(() => true), signal.then(() => false)]);
184	      if (!race) { delay.cancel(); return false; }
185	    }
186	  }
187	
188	  return true;
189	}
190	
191	// --- executeSteps (within an iteration) ---
192	
193	async function executeSteps(
194	  instanceId: string,
195	  iteration: number,
196	  ctx: TaskExecutionContext,
197	  signal: Promise<void>,
198	): Promise<boolean> {
199	  const { steps } = ctx.definition;
200	  const inst = ctx.state.getInstance(instanceId);
201	  if (!inst) return false;
202	  const completedInIter = inst.stepResults
203	    .filter((r) => r.iteration === iteration)
204	    .map((r) => r.stepIndex);
205	  const startIndex = completedInIter.length;
206	
207	  for (let i = startIndex; i < steps.length; i++) {
208	    const current = ctx.state.getInstance(instanceId);
209	    if (!current || current.lifecycle !== 'running') return false;
210	
211	    ctx.state.updateInstance(instanceId, { currentStepIndex: i });
212	
213	    const step = steps[i]!;
214	
215	    // For send steps with repeat, use executeRepeatableSend
216	    if (step.kind === 'send' && step.config.repeat) {
217	      const success = await executeRepeatableSend(ctx, instanceId, step, iteration, i, ctx.definition, signal);
218	      if (!success) return false;
219	    } else {
220	      const outcome = await executeStepCore(instanceId, step, iteration, i, ctx, signal);
221	      if (outcome === null) return false;
222	      ctx.state.addStepResult(instanceId, outcome.result);
223	      if (outcome.shouldStop) return false;
224	    }
225	
226	    // intervalAfterMs for send steps
227	    if (step.kind === 'send' && step.config.intervalAfterMs) {
228	      const delay = sleep(step.config.intervalAfterMs);
229	      const race = await Promise.race([
230	        delay.promise.then(() => 'done' as const),
231	        signal.then(() => 'interrupted' as const),
232	      ]);
233	      if (race === 'interrupted') {
234	        delay.cancel();
235	        return false;
236	      }
237	    }
238	  }
239	  return true;

240	}
241	
242	// --- executeStepCore ---
243	
244	async function executeStepCore(
245	  instanceId: string,
246	  step: TaskDefinition['steps'][number],
247	  iteration: number,
248	  stepIndex: number,
249	  ctx: TaskExecutionContext,
250	  signal: Promise<void>,
251	): Promise<{ result: TaskStepResult; shouldStop: boolean } | null> {
252	  switch (step.kind) {
253	    case 'send': {
254	      const result = await ctx.stepExecutors.executeSendStep(instanceId, step, iteration, stepIndex, ctx.definition);
255	      if (result.sendResult.kind !== 'sent') {
256	        return ctx.errorPolicy.applyErrorPolicy(instanceId, result, ctx.definition.errorPolicy, step, iteration, stepIndex, ctx.definition, signal);
257	      }
258	      return { result, shouldStop: false };
259	    }
260	
261	    case 'wait-condition': {
262	      const { result, interrupted } = await ctx.stepExecutors.executeWaitConditionStep(
263	        instanceId, step, iteration, stepIndex, signal,
264	      );
265	      if (interrupted) return null;
266	
267	      if (!result.matched && result.timedOut) {
268	        switch (step.config.onTimeout) {
269	          case 'continue':
270	            return { result, shouldStop: false };
271	          case 'skip':
272	            return { result: { ...result, appliedPolicy: 'skip-step' }, shouldStop: false };
273	          case 'fail':
274	            return ctx.errorPolicy.applyErrorPolicy(instanceId, result, ctx.definition.errorPolicy, step, iteration, stepIndex, ctx.definition, signal);
275	        }
276	      }
277	      return { result, shouldStop: false };
278	    }
279	
280	    case 'delay': {
281	      const { result, interrupted } = await ctx.stepExecutors.executeDelayStep(
282	        iteration, stepIndex, step.config.durationMs, signal,
283	      );
284	      if (interrupted) return null;
285	      return { result, shouldStop: false };
286	    }
287	  }
288	}
289	
290	// --- runTask (unified loop) ---
291	
292	export async function runTask(
293	  instanceId: string,
294	  ctx: TaskExecutionContext,
295	  signal: Promise<void>,
296	): Promise<void> {
297	  const driver = createDriver(ctx.definition.schedule, ctx, instanceId, signal);
298	  const stopGuard = createStopGuard(ctx.resolvedStop, ctx);
299	
300	  let iteration = ctx.state.getInstance(instanceId)?.currentIteration ?? 0;
301	
302	  while (!stopGuard.shouldStop(instanceId, iteration)) {
303	    const triggered = await driver.waitForNext(instanceId, iteration, signal);
304	    if (!triggered) break;
305	
306	    const completed = await executeSteps(instanceId, iteration, ctx, signal);
307	    if (!completed) break;
308	
309	    if (stopGuard.checkExitCondition()) break;
310	
311	    iteration++;
312	    // Only update iteration if the next iteration might execute
313	    if (!stopGuard.shouldStop(instanceId, iteration)) {
314	      ctx.state.updateInstance(instanceId, { currentIteration: iteration });
315	    }
316	  }
317	
318	  driver.dispose();
319	}

17	function makeErrorResult(kind: 'transport-error' | 'timeout' | 'target-unavailable' | 'build-error' = 'transport-error'): SendResult {

21	function createTestSetup(overrides: { definition?: TaskDefinition; sendResults?: readonly SendResult[] } = {}) {

23	  const fakeReceive = createFakeReceiveEventSource();

25	    sendService: fakeSend,
26	    receiveEventSource: fakeReceive,
27	    now: () => '2026-05-06T12:00:00.000Z',
28	  });
29	  const definition = overrides.definition ?? timedTaskDef();
30	  const instance = service.createTask(definition);
31	  return { service, fakeSend, fakeReceive, definition, instance };
32	}
33	
34	async function settle(service: TaskService, instanceId: string, timeoutMs = 2000): Promise<void> {
35	  const deadline = Date.now() + timeoutMs;
36	  while (Date.now() < deadline) {
37	    const inst = service.getInstance(instanceId);
38	    if (!inst || inst.lifecycle === 'completed' || inst.lifecycle === 'stopped' || inst.lifecycle === 'failed' || inst.lifecycle === 'paused') {
39	      return;
40	    }
41	    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 5));
42	  }
43	}
44

46	// Timed task
47	// ========================================================================

1	import type { TaskDefinition, TaskStepResult } from '../core';
2	import { isStepResultFailed } from '../core';
3	import type { TaskStateContainer } from '../state/task-state';
4	import type { ConditionRegistry } from './condition-registry';
5	import type { UpdateLifecycleFn } from './ta[REDACTED_SK]';
6	import type { createStepExecutors } from './task-step-executors';
7	import { sleep } from './task-step-executors';
8	
9	// --- Error policy context ---
10	
11	export interface ErrorPolicyContext {
12	  state: TaskStateContainer;
13	  conditionRegistry: ConditionRegistry;
14	  updateLifecycle: UpdateLifecycleFn;
15	  stepExecutors: ReturnType<typeof createStepExecutors>;
16	  now: () => string;
17	}
18	
19	export function createErrorPolicyHandler(ctx: ErrorPolicyContext) {
20	  async function applyErrorPolicy(
21	    instanceId: string,
22	    failedResult: TaskStepResult,
23	    policy: TaskDefinition['errorPolicy'],
24	    step: TaskDefinition['steps'][number],
25	    iteration: number,
26	    stepIndex: number,
27	    definition: TaskDefinition,
28	    signal: Promise<void>,
29	  ): Promise<{ result: TaskStepResult; shouldStop: boolean } | null> {
30	    switch (policy.onFailure) {
31	      case 'stop':
32	        ctx.updateLifecycle(instanceId, 'stop');
33	        return { result: { ...failedResult, appliedPolicy: 'stop' }, shouldStop: true };
34	
35	      case 'pause':
36	        ctx.updateLifecycle(instanceId, 'pause');
37	        return { result: { ...failedResult, appliedPolicy: 'pause' }, shouldStop: true };
38	
39	      case 'skip-step':
40	        return { result: { ...failedResult, appliedPolicy: 'skip-step' }, shouldStop: false };
41	
42	      case 'retry': {
43	        const maxRetries = policy.retryCount ?? 0;
44	        for (let attempt = 0; attempt < maxRetries; attempt++) {
45	          if (policy.retryDelayMs && policy.retryDelayMs > 0) {
46	            const retryDelay = sleep(policy.retryDelayMs);
47	            const race = await Promise.race([
48	              retryDelay.promise.then(() => false),
49	              signal.then(() => true),
50	            ]);
51	            if (race) {
52	              retryDelay.cancel();
53	              return null;
54	            }
55	          }
56	
57	          const inst = ctx.state.getInstance(instanceId);
58	          if (!inst || inst.lifecycle !== 'running') return null;
59	
60	          // Re-execute raw step without re-entering error policy
61	          let retryResult: TaskStepResult;
62	          switch (step.kind) {
63	            case 'send':
64	              retryResult = await ctx.stepExecutors.executeSendStep(instanceId, step, iteration, stepIndex, definition);
65	              break;
66	            case 'wait-condition': {
67	              const wc = await ctx.stepExecutors.executeWaitConditionStep(instanceId, step, iteration, stepIndex, signal);
68	              if (wc.interrupted) return null;
69	              retryResult = wc.result;
70	              break;
71	            }
72	            case 'delay': {
73	              const d = await ctx.stepExecutors.executeDelayStep(iteration, stepIndex, step.config.durationMs, signal);
74	              if (d.interrupted) return null;
75	              retryResult = d.result;
76	              break;
77	            }
78	          }
79	
80	          if (!isStepResultFailed(retryResult)) {
81	            return { result: retryResult, shouldStop: false };
82	          }
83	        }
84	        // Exhausted retries
85	        ctx.updateLifecycle(instanceId, 'fail', { error: 'Retry exhausted' });
86	        return { result: { ...failedResult, appliedPolicy: 'retry' }, shouldStop: true };
87	      }
88	    }
89	  }
90	
91	  return { applyErrorPolicy };
92	}

193	async function executeSteps(
194	  instanceId: string,
195	  iteration: number,
196	  ctx: TaskExecutionContext,
197	  signal: Promise<void>,
198	): Promise<boolean> {
199	  const { steps } = ctx.definition;
200	  const inst = ctx.state.getInstance(instanceId);
201	  if (!inst) return false;
202	  const completedInIter = inst.stepResults
203	    .filter((r) => r.iteration === iteration)
204	    .map((r) => r.stepIndex);
205	  const startIndex = completedInIter.length;
206	
207	  for (let i = startIndex; i < steps.length; i++) {
208	    const current = ctx.state.getInstance(instanceId);
209	    if (!current || current.lifecycle !== 'running') return false;
210	
211	    ctx.state.updateInstance(instanceId, { currentStepIndex: i });
212	
213	    const step = steps[i]!;
214	
215	    // For send steps with repeat, use executeRepeatableSend
216	    if (step.kind === 'send' && step.config.repeat) {
217	      const success = await executeRepeatableSend(ctx, instanceId, step, iteration, i, ctx.definition, signal);
218	      if (!success) return false;
219	    } else {
220	      const outcome = await executeStepCore(instanceId, step, iteration, i, ctx, signal);
221	      if (outcome === null) return false;
222	      ctx.state.addStepResult(instanceId, outcome.result);
223	      if (outcome.shouldStop) return false;
224	    }
225	
226	    // intervalAfterMs for send steps
227	    if (step.kind === 'send' && step.config.intervalAfterMs) {
228	      const delay = sleep(step.config.intervalAfterMs);
229	      const race = await Promise.race([
230	        delay.promise.then(() => 'done' as const),
231	        signal.then(() => 'interrupted' as const),
232	      ]);
233	      if (race === 'interrupted') {
234	        delay.cancel();
235	        return false;
236	      }
237	    }
238	  }
239	  return true;
240	}
241	
242	// --- executeStepCore ---
243	
244	async function executeStepCore(
245	  instanceId: string,
246	  step: TaskDefinition['steps'][number],
247	  iteration: number,
248	  stepIndex: number,
249	  ctx: TaskExecutionContext,
250	  signal: Promise<void>,
251	): Promise<{ result: TaskStepResult; shouldStop: boolean } | null> {
252	  switch (step.kind) {
253	    case 'send': {
254	      const result = await ctx.stepExecutors.executeSendStep(instanceId, step, iteration, stepIndex, ctx.definition);
255	      if (result.sendResult.kind !== 'sent') {
256	        return ctx.errorPolicy.applyErrorPolicy(instanceId, result, ctx.definition.errorPolicy, step, iteration, stepIndex, ctx.definition, signal);
257	      }
258	      return { result, shouldStop: false };
259	    }
260	
261	    case 'wait-condition': {
262	      const { result, interrupted } = await ctx.stepExecutors.executeWaitConditionStep(
263	        instanceId, step, iteration, stepIndex, signal,
264	      );
265	      if (interrupted) return null;
266	
267	      if (!result.matched && result.timedOut) {
268	        switch (step.config.onTimeout) {
269	          case 'continue':
270	            return { result, shouldStop: false };
271	          case 'skip':
272	            return { result: { ...result, appliedPolicy: 'skip-step' }, shouldStop: false };
273	          case 'fail':
274	            return ctx.errorPolicy.applyErrorPolicy(instanceId, result, ctx.definition.errorPolicy, step, iteration, stepIndex, ctx.definition, signal);
275	        }
276	      }
277	      return { result, shouldStop: false };
278	    }
279	
280	    case 'delay': {
281	      const { result, interrupted } = await ctx.stepExecutors.executeDelayStep(
282	        iteration, stepIndex, step.config.durationMs, signal,
283	      );
284	      if (interrupted) return null;
285	      return { result, shouldStop: false };
286	    }
287	  }
288	}

> AGENT

问题清楚了。`sequenceTaskDef()` 有 3 个 step：send → delay(500ms) → send。skip-step 测试给了 2 个 send result（error, sent）。

第一个 send 失败 → skip-step policy → `shouldStop: false` → 继续。第二个 step 是 delay(500ms)。第三个 step 是 send，消耗第二个 result(sent)。

但 `fakeSend` 返回结果队列式消费。问题可能是 delay step 完成后，第二个 send 消费 `sentResult` 但任务仍在 `running`。

> AGENT

delay 是 500ms。skip-step 测试的 settle timeout 是 2000ms，应该够了。问题可能在于 `createTestSetup` 里的 `createTaskService` 不传 `state`，所以每个 test 没有独立 state。

让我仔细看测试流程：测试创建 service → createTask → startTask → settle。service 内部 `startTask` 会异步执行 runTask。但 `createTestSetup` 没有 `afterEach` 清理。

实际上这个测试的问题是：它使用的 `sequenceTaskDef()` 有 500ms delay。在并发运行时，如果 `settle` 轮询间隔和 delay 交错，可能导致 settle 到 2000ms 超时时任务还没完成。

但 2000ms 应该足够 500ms delay + 2 次 send。让我看看 `createTaskService` 的 `startTask` 实现。

> AGENT

测试明确说 `lifecycle` 是 `running` 而不是 `completed`。这意味着任务执行到了某一步后卡住了。

让我仔细追踪执行流程：
1. `sequenceTaskDef()` = immediate, 3 steps: send → delay(500ms) → send
2. `errorPolicy: skipStep()` = `onFailure: 'skip-step'`
3. send results: [errorResult, sentResult]

Step 1 (send, frame-1) → executeSendStep → returns errorResult → `sendResult.kind !== 'sent'` → applyErrorPolicy → `skip-step` → returns `{ result: {..., appliedPolicy: 'skip-step'}, shouldStop: false }` → back to `executeStepCore` → returns `{ result, shouldStop: false }` → back to `executeSteps` → `outcome.shouldStop` is `false` → continues

Step 2 (delay, 500ms) → executeDelayStep → waits 500ms → returns `{ result, shouldStop: false }` → continues

Step 3 (send, frame-2) → executeSendStep → should consume `sentResult` → success

但等等，fakeSend 是怎么分配 results 的？

}\nfunction makeErrorResult(): SendResult { return makeSendResult({ kind: 'transport-error' }) as SendResult; }\n\nasync function settle(service: TaskService, instanceId: string, timeoutMs = 5000): Promise<void> {\n  const deadline = Date.now() + timeoutMs;\n  while (Date.now() < deadline) {\n    const inst = service.getInstance(instanceId);\n    if (!inst || inst.lifecycle === 'completed' || inst.lifecycle === 'stopped' || inst.lifecycle === 'failed' || inst.lifecycle === 'paused') {\n      return;\n    }\n    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 10));\n  }\n  // Debug: log current state\n  const inst = service.getInstance(instanceId);\n  console.log('SETTLE TIMEOUT. lifecycle:', inst?.lifecycle, 'stepResults:', JSON.stringify(inst?.stepResults));\n}\n\ndescribe('skip-step debug', () => {\n  it('skip-step policy continues to next step', async () => {\n    const def: TaskDefinition = {\n      ...sequenceTaskDef(),\n      errorPolicy: errorPolicies.skipStep(),\n    };\n    const results = [makeErrorResult(), makeSentResult()];\n    const fakeSend = createFakeSendService({ results });\n    const fakeReceive = createFakeReceiveEventSource();\n    const service = createTaskService({\n      sendService: fakeSend,\n      receiveEventSource: fakeReceive,\n      now: () => '2026-05-06T12:00:00.000Z',\n    });\n    const instance = service.createTask(def);\n\n    service.startTask(instance.instanceId);\n    await settle(service, instance.instanceId, 5000);\n\n    const final = service.getInstance(instance.instanceId);\n    console.log('lifecycle:', final?.lifecycle);\n    console.log('send calls:', fakeSend.calls.length);\n    console.log('stepResults:', JSON.stringify(final?.stepResults));\n    expect(final?.lifecycle).toBe('completed');\n    expect(fakeSend.calls.length).toBe(2);\n  });\n});\nTSEOF\ncp /tmp/test-skip-debug.ts src/features/task/__tests__/skip-step-debug.spec.ts\npnpm vitest run src/features/task/__tests__/skip-step-debug.spec.ts 2>&1 | tail -25",

> AGENT

确认是 flaky test。3 次跑，1 次失败 2 次通过。问题出在 async 时序。

根本原因：`sequenceTaskDef()` 包含 delay(500ms)，加上之前的测试可能留下事件循环竞争。最简单的修法：把测试中 sequenceTaskDef 的 delay 缩短到 10ms，或用不含 delay 的自定义 def。

> AGENT

单独过。问题出在跨文件并发。trigger 测试依赖 `fakeReceive.emit` 和事件循环，当并发跑多个测试文件时，如果有其他测试的异步操作泄漏，会影响 event subscription 的建立。

错误类型是 `STACK_TRACE_ERROR` 不是 assertion error — 很可能是前面某个测试泄漏了一个未处理的 promise rejection。

让我检查一下哪些测试文件会泄漏异步操作。

> AGENT

没有指定 pool，默认用 threads。在 threads 模式下所有测试共享事件循环，导致异步操作泄漏。加 `pool: 'forks'` 可以完全隔离。

import type {
  ConnectionCounterSnapshot,
  ConnectionRuntimeFact,
  ConnectionStateSnapshot,
  ReadonlyConnectionRuntimeFact,
  ReadonlyConnectionStateSnapshot,
  ReadonlyTransportConfig,
  ReadonlyTransportEventSnapshot,
  ReadonlyTransportTargetSnapshot,
  TransportConfig,
  TransportErrorSnapshot,
  TransportEventSnapshot,
  TransportTargetSnapshot,
} from './types';

function deepClone<T>(value: T): T {
  return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value));
}

export function cloneTransportConfig(config: ReadonlyTransportConfig): TransportConfig {
  return deepClone(config);
}

export function cloneTransportConfigs(
  configs: readonly ReadonlyTransportConfig[],
): TransportConfig[] {
  return configs.map(cloneTransportConfig);
}

export function cloneConnectionCounters(
  counters: Readonly<ConnectionCounterSnapshot>,
): ConnectionCounterSnapshot {
  return deepClone(counters);
}

export function cloneTransportTarget(
  target: ReadonlyTransportTargetSnapshot,
): TransportTargetSnapshot {
  return deepClone(target);
}

export function cloneTransportError(
  error: Readonly<TransportErrorSnapshot>,
): TransportErrorSnapshot {
  return deepClone(error);
}

export function cloneTransportEvent(
  event: ReadonlyTransportEventSnapshot,
): TransportEventSnapshot {
  return deepClone(event);
}

export function cloneConnectionRuntimeFact(
  fact: ReadonlyConnectionRuntimeFact,
): ConnectionRuntimeFact {
  return deepClone(fact);
}

export function cloneConnectionStateSnapshot(
  snapshot: ReadonlyConnectionStateSnapshot,
): ConnectionStateSnapshot {
  return deepClone(snapshot);
}
```
